# E 開頭詞條

<a id="entry-e-000001"></a>
## E

**發音：** /ɛ/
*prop. n.* 1. **誒話 /誒話, 五色話 /五色話** — A Tai-Chinese mixed language spoken primarily in Rongshui Miao Autonomous County, Guangxi, China; Kjang E.

<a id="entry-e-000002"></a>
## e

*n.* 1. **鎄** — a radioactive transuranic element produced by bombarding plutonium with neutrons
2. **東, 東方** — the cardinal compass point that is at 90 degrees
3. **自然對數的底** — the base of the natural system of logarithms; approximately equal to 2.718282...
4. a fat-soluble vitamin that is essential for normal reproduction; an important antioxidant that neutralizes free radicals in the body
5. the 5th letter of the Roman alphabet

<a id="entry-e-000003"></a>
## E major

**發音：** /ˈiː ˈmeɪ.dʒə(ɹ)/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **E大調 /E大調** — The major key with the notes E, F♯, G♯, A, B, C♯, D♯, the key signature of which has four sharps.

<a id="entry-e-000004"></a>
## E minor

**發音：** /ˈiː ˈmaɪ.nə(ɹ)/

<a id="entry-e-000005"></a>
## e pluribus unum

*phr.* 1. **合眾為一 /合眾為一** — "out of many, one" or "from many, one" (alternatively translated as "one out of many" or "one from many”)

<a id="entry-e-000006"></a>
## e-banking

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電子銀行 /電子銀行** — Banking carried out electronically, as for example over the Internet.

<a id="entry-e-000007"></a>
## e-bike

**發音：** /ˈiː.baɪk/

## e-bomb

*n.* 1. **電子炸彈, 電磁炸彈** — a bomb that explodes in midair and releases a massive burst of electromagnetic energy sufficient to disable computers and telecommunications without killing people or damaging buildings

## e-book

2. commerce conducted electronically (as on the internet)

<a id="entry-e-000011"></a>

*n.* 1. **電子詞典 /電子詞典** — A dictionary in electronic form (software or hardware).

## e-mail address

*n.* 1. **電子郵件地址 /電子郵件地址, 伊妹兒地址 /伊妹兒地址** — A unique identifier of the form user name@domain name specifying a virtual location to which e-mail can be sent.

<a id="entry-e-000018"></a>
## e-mailbox

*n.* 1. **電子郵箱 /電子郵箱** — A repository for electronic mail.

*n.* 1. **電子訊息 /電子訊息** — A message sent by computer.

## e-mycin

<a id="entry-e-000021"></a>
## e-paper

<a id="entry-e-000022"></a>
## e-reader

**發音：** /ˈiˌɹidɚ/
*n.* 1. **電子閱讀器 /電子閱讀器, 電紙書 /電紙書, 墨水屏** — A hardware device or software program used to read e-books.

<a id="entry-e-000024"></a>
## e-waste

**發音：** /ˈiːweɪst/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電子廢棄物 /電子廢棄物, 電子垃圾 /電子垃圾** — Discarded electrical and electronic equipment.

<a id="entry-e-000025"></a>
## e.g.

**發音：** /ˌiːˈdʒiː/
*adv.* 1. **例如** — An initialism used to introduce an illustrative example or short list of examples: for the sake of an example; for example.
2. as an example
*“take ribbon snakes, for example”*

<a id="entry-e-000026"></a>
## each

**發音：** /iːt͡ʃ/
*adj.* 1. **每一個+的** — (used of count nouns) every one considered individually
*“each person is mortal”*
*“each party is welcome”*
*adv.* 1. to or from every one of two or more (considered individually)
*“they received $10 each”*
*det.* 1. **每, 各, 每隔** — All; every; qualifying a singular noun, indicating all examples of the thing so named seen as individual or separate items (compare every).

<a id="entry-e-000027"></a>
## each other

**發音：** /iːˈt͡ʃʌð.ə/
*pron.* 1. *(pronoun, reciprocal)* **互相, 彼此, 交互** — To one another; one to the other; signifies that a verb applies to two or more entities both as subjects and as direct objects

<a id="entry-e-000028"></a>
## eacles

*n.* 1. **帝蛾屬** — imperial moths

<a id="entry-e-000029"></a>
## eacles imperialis

*n.* 1. **帝蛾** — large American moth having yellow wings with purplish or brownish markings; larvae feed on e.g. maple and pine trees

<a id="entry-e-000030"></a>
## eager

**發音：** /ˈiɡɚ/
*adj.* 1. **熱切+的** — having or showing keen interest or intense desire or impatient expectancy
*“eager to learn”*
*“eager to travel abroad”*
2. **急於 /急於** — Not employing lazy evaluation; calculating results immediately, rather than deferring calculation until they are required.
*n.* 1. a high wave (often dangerous) caused by tidal flow (as by colliding tidal currents or in a narrow estuary)

<a id="entry-e-000031"></a>
## eagle

**發音：** /ˈiːɡəl/
*n.* 1. **老鷹, 鷹** — any of various large keen-sighted diurnal birds of prey noted for their broad wings and strong soaring flight
2. **老鷹 /老鷹, 鷹 /鷹** — Any of several large carnivorous and carrion-eating birds in the family Accipitridae, having a powerful hooked bill and keen vision.
3. **老鷹球** — (golf) a score of two strokes under par on a hole
4. **銀鷹徽金幣** — a former gold coin in the United States worth 10 dollars
5. **權利的象徵, 鷹狀標誌** — an emblem representing power
*“the Roman eagle”*
*v.* 1. shoot two strokes under par
*“She eagled the hole”*
2. shoot in two strokes under par

<a id="entry-e-000032"></a>
## eagle owl

**發音：** /ˈiːɡəl ˈɑʊl/
*n.* 1. **鵰鴞 /雕鴞** — Any of various Old World large owls of the genus Bubo, especially Eurasian eagle owls (Bubo bubo), that have prominent ear tufts.

<a id="entry-e-000033"></a>
## eagle-eyed

*adj.* 1. **眼力好+的** — capable of seeing to a great distance

<a id="entry-e-000034"></a>
## eaglet

*n.* 1. **小鷹 /小鷹** — The immature young of an eagle; an eagle chick.
2. a young eagle

<a id="entry-e-000035"></a>
## eames chair

*n.* 1. **埃姆斯椅** — a chair designed by Charles Eames; originally made of molded plywood; seat and back shaped to fit the human body

<a id="entry-e-000036"></a>
## ear

**發音：** /ˈɪə̯/
*n.* 1. **耳朵** — the sense organ for hearing and equilibrium
2. *(countable)* **耳朵, 耳** — The organ of hearing, consisting of the pinna or auricle, auditory canal, eardrum, malleus, incus, stapes and cochlea.
3. **穗, 耳穗, 頭狀花序** — fruiting spike of a cereal plant especially corn
4. good hearing
*“he had a keen ear”*
*“a good ear for pitch”*
5. the externally visible cartilaginous structure of the external ear
6. attention to what is said
*“he tried to get her ear”*

<a id="entry-e-000037"></a>
## ear canal

*n.* 1. **耳道** — The tube or meatus running from the outer ear to the eardrum through which sound enters.
2. either of the passages in the outer ear from the auricle to the tympanic membrane

<a id="entry-e-000038"></a>
## ear fungus

*n.* 1. **黑木耳, 光木耳** — widely distributed edible fungus shaped like a human ear and growing on decaying wood

<a id="entry-e-000039"></a>
## ear lobe

**發音：** /ɪəɹ ləʊb/
*n.* 1. **耳垂** — The lower, exterior, fleshy, bulbous part of the human ear.
2. the fleshy pendulous part of the external human ear

<a id="entry-e-000040"></a>
## ear tag

*v.* 1. **耳標 /耳標** — To apply an ear tag to an animal.

<a id="entry-e-000041"></a>
## ear trumpet

*n.* 1. **喇叭狀助聽器** — a conical acoustic device formerly used to direct sound to the ear of a hearing-impaired person

<a id="entry-e-000042"></a>
## ear-like

*adj.* 1. **耳狀+的** — having a shape resembling an ear

<a id="entry-e-000043"></a>
## ear-shaped

*adj.* 1. **耳狀+的** — having a shape resembling an ear

<a id="entry-e-000044"></a>
## ear-shell

*n.* 1. **大鮑, 鮑, 鮑貝, 鮑魚** — any of various large edible marine gastropods of the genus Haliotis having an ear-shaped shell with pearly interior

<a id="entry-e-000045"></a>
## earache

**發音：** /ˈɪə̯ɹeɪk/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **耳痛** — A pain in the middle or inner ear.
2. **耳痛** — an ache localized in the middle or inner ear

<a id="entry-e-000046"></a>
## earbud

**發音：** /ˈiɹ.bʌd/
*n.* 1. **耳塞式耳機 /耳塞式耳機** — A small earphone designed to be placed in the ear canal for use with portable sound systems.

<a id="entry-e-000047"></a>
## eardrop

*n.* 1. *(dated)* **耳環 /耳環** — A pendant for the ear; an earring.
2. an earring with a pendant ornament

<a id="entry-e-000048"></a>
## eardrum

**發音：** /ˈɪəˌdɹʌm/
*n.* 1. **鼓膜, 耳鼓, 耳膜** — A thin membrane that separates the outer ear from the middle ear and transmits sound from the air to the malleus.
2. the membrane in the ear that vibrates to sound

<a id="entry-e-000049"></a>
## eared

*adj.* 1. **卷角的** — worn or shabby from overuse or (of pages) from having corners turned down
*“a somewhat dog-eared duke...a bit run down"-Clifton Fadiman”*
*“an old book with dog-eared pages”*
2. having ears (or appendages resembling ears) or having ears of a specified kind; often used in combination

<a id="entry-e-000050"></a>
## eared grebe

*n.* 1. **黑頸鸊鷉** — small grebe with yellow ear tufts and a black neck; found in Eurasia and southern Africa as well as western United States

<a id="entry-e-000051"></a>
## eared seal

*n.* 1. **海獅 /海獅, 海狗** — A seal belonging in the Otariidae family; a fur seal or a sea lion, differing from the true seals in having small, visible ears and the ability to walk on land.
2. **小耳朵海豹, 海熊, 海狗** — pinniped mammal having external ear flaps and hind limbs used for locomotion on land; valued for its soft underfur

<a id="entry-e-000052"></a>
## earflap

*n.* 1. **帽耳, 耳扇, 耳罩** — one of two flaps attached to a cap to keep the ears warm

<a id="entry-e-000053"></a>
## eargasmic

*adj.* 1. *(slang)* **懷孕 /懷孕** — Causing an eargasm; giving great musical pleasure.

<a id="entry-e-000054"></a>
## earl

**發音：** /ɜːl/
*n.* 1. **伯爵** — A British or Irish nobleman next in rank above a viscount and below a marquess; equivalent to a European count. A female using the style is termed a countess.
2. a British peer ranking below a marquess and above a viscount

<a id="entry-e-000055"></a>
## Earl Grey

*n.* 1. *(countable, usually)* **伯爵茶** — A serving of this kind of tea.

<a id="entry-e-000056"></a>
## earlap

*n.* 1. **帽耳, 耳扇, 耳罩** — one of two flaps attached to a cap to keep the ears warm

<a id="entry-e-000057"></a>
## earldom

*n.* 1. **伯爵爵位, 伯爵的身分** — the dignity or rank or position of an earl or countess
2. the domain controlled by an earl or count or countess

<a id="entry-e-000058"></a>
## earless seal

*n.* 1. **海豹** — Synonym of true seal.
2. **海豹, 粗毛海豹** — any of several seals lacking external ear flaps and having a stiff hairlike coat with hind limbs reduced to swimming flippers

<a id="entry-e-000059"></a>
## early

**發音：** /ˈɜːli/
*adj.* 1. **在初期+的, 早+的, 早期+的** — at or near the beginning of a period of time or course of events or before the usual or expected time
*“early morning”*
*“an early warning”*
2. **早** — At a time in advance of the usual or expected event.
3. being or occurring at an early stage of development
*“in an early stage”*
*“early forms of life”*
4. belonging to the distant past
*“the early inhabitants of Europe”*
*“former generations”*
5. very young
*“at an early age”*
6. of an early stage in the development of a language or literature
*“the Early Hebrew alphabetical script is that used mainly from the 11th to the 6th centuries B.C.”*
*“Early Modern English is represented in documents printed from 1476 to 1700”*
*adv.* 1. **早** — At a time before expected; sooner than usual.
2. during an early stage
*“early on in her career”*
3. before the usual time or the time expected
*“she graduated early”*
*“the house was completed ahead of time”*
4. in good time
*“he awoke betimes that morning”*

<a id="entry-e-000060"></a>
## early bird

*n.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **早起者, 早到者, 早鳥 /早鳥** — A person who wakes early or arrives early, typically before most others.
2. a person who arrives early before others do
3. a person who gets up very early in the morning

<a id="entry-e-000061"></a>
## early childhood

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **兒童早期 /兒童早期** — A stage in human development, generally including toddlerhood and some time afterwards.
2. the early stage of growth or development

<a id="entry-e-000062"></a>
## early childhood education

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **兒童早期教育學 /兒童早期教育學, 兒童早期教育 /兒童早期教育, 早期教育, 學前教育 /學前教育, 幼教, 幼兒教育 /幼兒教育, 早教** — The formal teaching and care of young children by people other than their family, or in settings outside of the home, before the age of normal schooling.

<a id="entry-e-000063"></a>
## early days

*n.* 1. **早期** — an early period of development
*“during the youth of the project”*

<a id="entry-e-000064"></a>
## Early Modern Czech

*prop. n.* 1. **近代捷克語 /近代捷克語** — A historical form of the Czech language used between 1780 to the 1840s.

<a id="entry-e-000065"></a>
## Early Modern English

*prop. n.* 1. **近代英語 /近代英語** — The form of the English language written and spoken from the end of the 15th century to the mid-17th century.

<a id="entry-e-000066"></a>
## Early Modern Korean

*prop. n.* 1. **近代韓語 /近代韓語** — The Korean language from the beginning of the seventeenth century to the end of the nineteenth century.

<a id="entry-e-000067"></a>
## early morel

*n.* 1. **皺蓋鍾菌** — resembles a thimble on a finger; the surface of the fertile portion is folded into wrinkles that extend from the top down; fruiting begins in spring before the leaves are out on the trees

<a id="entry-e-000068"></a>
## early to bed, early to rise, makes a man healthy, wealthy and wise

*phr.* 1. **早睡早起，使人健康、富裕和聰穎 /早睡早起, 使人健康、富裕和聰穎** — A person who goes to bed early and wakes up early will lead a more successful life.

<a id="entry-e-000069"></a>
## early wake-robin

*n.* 1. **地珠, 大花延齡草, 頭頂一顆珠** — a low perennial white-flowered trillium found in the southeastern United States

<a id="entry-e-000070"></a>
## early warning radar

*n.* 1. **遠程警戒雷達** — a radar that is part of an early warning system

<a id="entry-e-000071"></a>
## early warning system

*n.* 1. **預警系統** — a network of radar installations designed to detect enemy missiles or aircraft while there is still time to intercept them

<a id="entry-e-000072"></a>
## early-morning hour

*n.* 1. **凌晨, 清早, 清晨, 早晨** — an hour early in the morning

<a id="entry-e-000073"></a>
## earmark

*n.* 1. identification mark on the ear of a domestic animal
2. a distinctive characteristic or attribute
*v.* 1. **指定用途** — give or assign a resource to a particular person or cause
*“I will earmark this money for your research”*
*“She sets aside time for meditation every day”*

<a id="entry-e-000074"></a>
## earmuff

**發音：** /ˈɪəmʌfs/
*n.* 1. **耳套, 耳罩, 耳包子** — A garment to keep the ears warm.
2. **禦寒耳罩, 耳套** — either of a pair of ear coverings (usually connected by a headband) that are worn to keep the ears warm in cold weather

<a id="entry-e-000075"></a>
## earn

**發音：** /ɜːn/
*v.* 1. **增益, 收穫, 獲利, 賺, 獲得** — earn on some commercial or business transaction; earn as salary or wages
*“How much do you make a month in your new job?”*
*“She earns a lot in her new job”*
2. **掙, 掙得, 賺, 賺取, 獲得** — acquire or deserve by one's efforts or actions
3. *(transitive)* **掙 /掙, 賺 /賺, 賺取 /賺取, 獲得 /獲得, 值得, 應受 /應受** — To gain (success, reward, recognition) through applied effort or work.

<a id="entry-e-000076"></a>
## earnest

**發音：** /ˈɝ.nɪst/
*adj.* 1. **不開玩笑+的, 莊重+的, 認真+的, 鄭重+的, 嚴肅+的** — characterized by a firm and humorless belief in the validity of your opinions
*“both sides were deeply in earnest, even passionate”*
*“an entirely sincere and cruel tyrant”*
2. **懇切+的, 衷心+的, 誠懇+的, 誠摯+的, 真誠+的** — earnest
*“one's dearest wish”*
*“devout wishes for their success”*
3. **熱心 /熱心** — Strenuous; diligent.
4. **認真 /認真, 嚴重 /嚴重, 沉重** — Serious; weighty; of a serious, weighty, or important nature; important.
5. not distracted by anything unrelated to the goal
*n.* 1. **抵押品** — something of value given by one person to another to bind a contract

<a id="entry-e-000077"></a>
## earnest money

*n.* 1. *(US, countable, uncountable)* **保證金 /保證金, 定金** — Money paid as a deposit to show intent to buy or to reserve an item to be purchased; especially, money accompanying an offer to buy real estate.
2. **定錢, 定金, 保證金** — money given by a buyer to a seller to bind a contract

<a id="entry-e-000078"></a>
## earning per share

*n.* 1. **每股平均收益** — the portion of a company's profit allocated to each outstanding share of common stock

<a id="entry-e-000079"></a>
## earnings

*n.* 1. **淨利, 利潤, 純利, 純利潤, 利益** — the excess of revenues over outlays in a given period of time (including depreciation and other non-cash expenses)
2. **作報償的東西, 工資, 報酬, 薪水, 薪金** — something that remunerates
*“wages were paid by check”*
*“he wasted his pay on drink”*
3. *(plural, plural-only)* **收益, 收入, 所得** — Wages, money earned, income.

<a id="entry-e-000080"></a>
## earnings call

*n.* 1. **業績說明會 /業績說明會** — A webcast or teleconference wherein the management of a listed company provides further explanation of its earnings reports to investors, analysts, and the media.

<a id="entry-e-000081"></a>
## earnings report

*n.* 1. **清算賬目, 賬目** — a financial statement that gives operating results for a specific period

<a id="entry-e-000082"></a>
## earphone

**發音：** /ˈɪə(ɹ)fəʊn/
*n.* 1. **受話器, 耳機** — electro-acoustic transducer for converting electric signals into sounds; it is held over or inserted into the ear
*“it was not the typing but the earphones that she disliked”*
2. **耳機 /耳機, 耳筒** — A transducer that converts electric signals into sound and is held near the ear, especially as part of a telephone; an earpiece or headphone.

<a id="entry-e-000083"></a>
## earphones

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **耳機 /耳機** — A pair of small loudspeakers worn inside each outer ear or covering all or part of the ear, without a connecting band worn over head.

<a id="entry-e-000084"></a>
## earpick

**發音：** /ˈɪəɹˌpɪk ~ ˈiəɹˌpɪk/

<a id="entry-e-000085"></a>

**發音：** /ˈɪəpiːs/
*n.* 1. **耳塞** — A speaker placed inside or held near to the ear.
2. **受話器, 耳機** — electro-acoustic transducer for converting electric signals into sounds; it is held over or inserted into the ear
*“it was not the typing but the earphones that she disliked”*

<a id="entry-e-000086"></a>
## earplug

**發音：** /ˈɪəplʌɡ/

2. **耳塞, 耳栓** — a plug of cotton, wax, or rubber that is fitted into the ear canal for protection against the entry of water or loud noise
3. an earphone that is inserted into the ear canal

<a id="entry-e-000087"></a>
## earring

**發音：** /ˈɪəɹɪŋ/
*n.* 1. **耳環 /耳環** — A piece of jewelry worn on the ear.
2. **耳環, 耳飾** — jewelry to ornament the ear; usually clipped to the earlobe or fastened through a hole in the lobe

<a id="entry-e-000088"></a>
## Earth

**發音：** /ɜːθ/
*prop. n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **地球** — The third planet of the Solar System; the world upon which humans live.

<a id="entry-e-000089"></a>
## earth

*n.* 1. **世界, 地球** — the 3rd planet from the sun; the planet we live on
*“the Earth moves around the sun”*
*“he sailed around the world”*
2. **泥土** — the loose soft material that makes up a large part of the land surface
*“they dug into the earth outside the church”*
3. **土地, 地, 地面, 陸地** — the solid part of the earth's surface
*“the plane turned away from the sea and moved back over land”*
*“the earth shook for several minutes”*
4. **土, 土元素** — once thought to be one of four elements composing the universe (Empedocles)
5. **接地** — a connection between an electrical device and a large conducting body, such as the earth (which is taken to be at zero voltage)
6. the abode of mortals (as contrasted with Heaven or Hell)
*“it was hell on earth”*
*v.* 1. **躲入洞內** — hide in the earth like a hunted animal
2. connect to the earth
*“earth the circuit”*

<a id="entry-e-000090"></a>
## earth color

*n.* 1. **礦物顏料** — a colored mineral used as a pigment

<a id="entry-e-000091"></a>
## Earth Day

*prop. n.* 1. *(North-American)* **世界地球日, 地球日** — April 22; Created in 1970, global day of observance of the need to protect the earth.

<a id="entry-e-000092"></a>
## earth science

*n.* 1. **地球科學 /地球科學** — Any of the sciences dealing with the planet Earth, such as geology and meteorology.
2. any of the sciences that deal with the earth or its parts

<a id="entry-e-000093"></a>
## earth tremor

*n.* 1. **微震** — A small earthquake, or seismic event.
2. a small earthquake

## earth-ball

*n.* 1. **塊菌, 無糧藤果, 松露, 豬拱菌, 隔山撬** — any of various highly prized edible subterranean fungi of the genus Tuber; grow naturally in southwestern Europe

<a id="entry-e-000096"></a>
## earth-tongue

<a id="entry-e-000097"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000098"></a>

2. confined to the earth
3. lacking wit or imagination
*“a pedestrian movie plot”*

<a id="entry-e-000099"></a>

2. **陶器** — ceramic ware made of porous clay fired at low heat

## earthly branch

*n.* 1. *(historical)* **地支, 十二支** — The (approximately) twelve years of Jupiter's orbital period, forming the Chinese zodiac and personified as twelve or sixty gods known as Tai Sui.

<a id="entry-e-000105"></a>
## earthnut

*n.* 1. **塊菌, 無糧藤果, 松露, 豬拱菌, 隔山撬** — any of various highly prized edible subterranean fungi of the genus Tuber; grow naturally in southwestern Europe
2. **地豆, 泥豆, 番豆, 落生, 長生果** — a common European plant having edible tubers with the flavor of roasted chestnuts
3. **花生, 落花生** — pod of the peanut vine containing usually 2 nuts or seeds; `groundnut' and `monkey nut' are British terms
4. edible subterranean fungus of the genus Tuber

<a id="entry-e-000106"></a>
## earthquake

**發音：** /ˈɜːθkweɪk/
*n.* 1. **地震** — shaking and vibration at the surface of the earth resulting from underground movement along a fault plane of from volcanic activity
2. **地震, 地動 /地動** — A shaking of the ground, caused by volcanic activity or movement around geologic faults.
3. **地震** — a disturbance that is extremely disruptive
*“selling the company caused an earthquake among the employees”*
*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **地震** — To undergo an earthquake.

<a id="entry-e-000107"></a>
## earthshaking

*adj.* 1. **驚天動地+的, 震撼世界+的** — sufficiently significant to affect the whole world
*“earthshaking proposals”*
*“the contest was no world-shaking affair”*
2. loud enough to shake the very earth

<a id="entry-e-000108"></a>
## earthshine

**發音：** /ˈɜːθʃaɪn/

<a id="entry-e-000109"></a>
## earthstar

## earthtongue

*n.* 1. **地舌菌** — any club-shaped fungus of the genus Geoglossum

2. **土木工事, 土木工程** — an earthen rampart

<a id="entry-e-000112"></a>
## earthworm

**發音：** /ˈɜː(ɹ)θˌwɜː(ɹ)m/
*n.* 1. **蚯蚓, 地龍 /地龍** — A worm that lives in the ground.
2. **蚯蚓** — terrestrial worm that burrows into and helps aerate soil; often surfaces when the ground is cool or wet; used as bait by anglers

<a id="entry-e-000113"></a>
## earthy

*adj.* 1. **腳踏實地+的, 務實+的** — sensible and practical
*“has a straightforward down-to-earth approach to a problem”*
*“her earthy common sense”*
2. conspicuously and tastelessly indecent
*“coarse language”*
*“a crude joke”*
3. not far removed from or suggestive of nature
*“the earthy taste of warm milk fresh from the cow”*
*“earthy smells of new-mown grass”*
4. hearty and lusty
*“an earthy enjoyment of life”*
5. of or consisting of or resembling earth
*“it had an earthy smell”*
*“only a little earthy bank separates me from the edge of the ocean”*

<a id="entry-e-000114"></a>
## earwax

**發音：** /ˈiɹ.wæks/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **耳屎, 耳垢, 耵聹 /耵聹** — A waxy substance secreted by the ear.
2. a soft yellow wax secreted by glands in the ear canal

<a id="entry-e-000115"></a>
## earwig

**發音：** /ˈɪə(ɹ)wɪɡ/
*n.* 1. **蠼螋** — Any of various insects of the order Dermaptera that have elongated bodies, large membranous wings folded underneath short leathery forewings and a pair of large pincers protruding from the rear of the abdomen.
2. **蠼螋** — any of numerous insects of the order Dermaptera having elongate bodies and slender many-jointed antennae and a pair of large pincers at the rear of the abdomen

<a id="entry-e-000116"></a>
## earworm

**發音：** /ˈɪəwɜːm/
*n.* 1. **耳蟲 /耳蟲** — A tune that keeps replaying in one's head or that one keeps thinking about, especially if unwanted.

<a id="entry-e-000117"></a>
## ease

*v.* 1. move gently or carefully
*“He eased himself into the chair”*
2. lessen pain or discomfort; alleviate
*“ease the pain in your legs”*
3. make easier
*“you could facilitate the process by sharing your knowledge”*
4. lessen the intensity of or calm
*“The news eased my conscience”*
*“still the fears”*
*n.* 1. **寬裕, 安逸, 舒適** — a freedom from financial difficulty that promotes a comfortable state
*“a life of luxury and ease”*
*“he had all the material comforts of this world”*
2. **悠閒, 解除, 舒適, 減輕** — the condition of being comfortable or relieved (especially after being relieved of distress)
*“he enjoyed his relief from responsibility”*
*“getting it off his conscience gave him some ease”*
3. freedom from difficulty or hardship or effort
*“he rose through the ranks with apparent ease”*
*“they put it into containers for ease of transportation”*
4. freedom from constraint or embarrassment
*“I am never at ease with strangers”*
5. freedom from activity (work or strain or responsibility)
*“took his repose by the swimming pool”*

<a id="entry-e-000118"></a>
## ease oneself

*v.* 1. *(euphemistic)* **方便, 方便一下** — To defecate or urinate.

<a id="entry-e-000119"></a>
## eased

*adj.* 1. **使緩和+的, 放鬆+的** — (of pain or sorrow) made easier to bear

<a id="entry-e-000120"></a>
## easel

**發音：** /ˈiː.z(ə)l/
*n.* 1. **畫架, 黑板架** — an upright tripod for displaying something (usually an artist's canvas)
2. **黑板架, 畫架 /畫架** — An upright frame, typically on three legs, for displaying or supporting something, such as an artist's canvas.

<a id="entry-e-000121"></a>
## easement

**發音：** /ˈiːzm(ə)nt/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **地役權** — An interest in land which grants the legal right to use another person's real property (real estate), generally in order to cross a part of the property or to gain access to something on the property (right of way).
2. **緩和, 減輕** — the act of reducing something unpleasant (as pain or annoyance)
*“he asked the nurse for relief from the constant pain”*
3. (law) the privilege of using something that is not your own (as using another's land as a right of way to your own land)

<a id="entry-e-000122"></a>
## easier said than done

*adj.* 1. *(comparative-only, idiomatic)* **談何容易 /談何容易, 說得倒輕巧 /說得倒輕巧** — Easy to propose, but difficult to accomplish.

<a id="entry-e-000123"></a>
## easily

**發音：** /ˈiː.zɪ.li/
*adv.* 1. **舒適地 /舒適地** — Comfortably, without discomfort or anxiety.
2. **容易地** — Without difficulty.
3. *(colloquial, not-comparable)* **毫無疑問地 /毫無疑問地** — Absolutely, without question.
4. with ease (`easy' is sometimes used informally for `easily')
*“she was easily excited”*
*“was easily confused”*
5. without question
*“easily the best book she's written”*
6. indicating high probability; in all likelihood
*“I might well do it”*
*“a mistake that could easily have ended in disaster”*

<a id="entry-e-000124"></a>
## easiness

*n.* 1. **放鬆, 休息** — a feeling of refreshing tranquility and an absence of tension or worry
*“the easiness we feel when sleeping”*
2. freedom from difficulty or hardship or effort
*“he rose through the ranks with apparent ease”*
*“they put it into containers for ease of transportation”*
3. the quality of being easy in behavior or style
*“there was an easiness between them”*
*“a natural easiness of manner”*

<a id="entry-e-000125"></a>
## easing

*n.* 1. **緩和, 減輕** — the act of reducing something unpleasant (as pain or annoyance)
*“he asked the nurse for relief from the constant pain”*
2. a change for the better

It is celebrated on the first Sunday (and Monday) following the full moon that occurs on or next after the vernal equinox, ranging in most of Western Christianity (such as Protestantism and Roman Catholicism) from March 22 to April 25, and in Eastern Christianity (such as the Coptic Church and Eastern Orthodox Church) from April 4 to May 8.

*adj.* 1. lying in or toward the east

<a id="entry-e-000167"></a>
## eastern

**發音：** /ˈiː.stən/
*adj.* 1. **東方的 /東方的, 東 /東** — Of, facing, situated in, or related to the east.
2. lying toward or situated in the east
*“the eastern end of the island”*
3. of or characteristic of eastern regions of the United States
*“the Eastern establishment”*
4. lying in or toward the east
*“the east side of New York”*
*“eastern cities”*
5. relating to or characteristic of regions of eastern parts of the world
*“Eastern Europe”*
*“the Eastern religions”*
6. from the east; used especially of winds
*“an eastern wind”*
*“the winds are easterly”*

<a id="entry-e-000192"></a>
## easy

**發音：** /ˈiːzi/
*adj.* 1. **容易+的, 簡單+的, 輕+的** — posing no difficulty; requiring little effort
*“an easy job”*
*“an easy problem”*
2. **容易, 簡單 /簡單** — Requiring little skill or effort.
3. *(derogatory, informal, usually)* **隨便 /隨便** — Consenting readily to sex.
4. **易上當+的** — readily exploited or tricked
*“an easy victim”*
*“an easy mark”*
5. **富裕+的, 手頭寬裕+的, 有錢+的, 富有+的** — in fortunate circumstances financially; moderately rich
*“they were comfortable or even wealthy by some standards”*
*“easy living”*
6. **毫無約束+的, 放蕩+的, 淫蕩+的** — casual and unrestrained in sexual behavior
*“her easy virtue”*
*“he was told to avoid loose (or light) women”*
*adv.* 1. with ease (`easy' is sometimes used informally for `easily')
*“she was easily excited”*
*“was easily confused”*
2. without speed (`slow' is sometimes used informally for `slowly')
*“he spoke slowly”*
*“go easy here--the road is slippery”*
3. in a relaxed manner; or without hardship
*“just wanted to take it easy" (`soft' is nonstandard)”*

<a id="entry-e-000193"></a>
## easy as pie

*adj.* 1. *(colloquial)* **易如反掌, 得來全不費功夫** — Very easy.

<a id="entry-e-000194"></a>

2. **安樂椅 /安樂椅** — A comfortable chair, often well upholstered; a lounge chair.

<a id="entry-e-000195"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000199"></a>
## eat

*v.* 1. **吃, 吃東西, 食** — take in solid food
*“She was eating a banana”*
*“What did you eat for dinner last night?”*
2. eat a meal; take a meal
*“We did not eat until 10 P.M. because there were so many phone calls”*
*“I didn't eat yet, so I gladly accept your invitation”*
3. take in food; used of animals only
*“This dog doesn't eat certain kinds of meat”*
*“What do whales eat?”*
4. worry or cause anxiety in a persistent way
*“What's eating you?”*
5. use up (resources or materials)
*“this car consumes a lot of gas”*
*“We exhausted our savings”*
6. cause to deteriorate due to the action of water, air, or an acid
*“The acid corroded the metal”*
*“The steady dripping of water rusted the metal stopper in the sink”*

<a id="entry-e-000200"></a>
## eat like a bird

*v.* 1. *(colloquial)* **吃得少** — To eat in small amounts rather than in a single full meal.

<a id="entry-e-000201"></a>
## eat one's fill

*v.* 1. **吃飽 /吃飽** — To eat to one's satisfaction, until full. (This entry is a translation hub.)

<a id="entry-e-000202"></a>
## eat one's hat

*v.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **名字倒過來寫 /名字倒過來寫** — Used to express absolute certainty that something will not happen or is untrue.

<a id="entry-e-000203"></a>
## eat one's heart out

<a id="entry-e-000204"></a>
## eat one's words

2. eat at a restaurant or at somebody else's home

3. use up (resources or materials)

*“the general of the Jesuits has several provinces under him”*

<a id="entry-e-000264"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000269"></a>

*prop. n.* 1. **鄂城** — A district of Ezhou, Hubei, China.

<a id="entry-e-000270"></a>
## echidna

**發音：** /əˈkɪdnə/
*n.* 1. **針鼴 /針鼴, 食蟻蝟 /食蟻蝟, 刺食蟻獸 /刺食蟻獸** — Any of the small spined monotremes in the family Tachyglossidae, the four extant species of which are found in Australia and southern New Guinea.
2. a burrowing monotreme mammal covered with spines and having a long snout and claws for hunting ants and termites; native to New Guinea
3. a burrowing monotreme mammal covered with spines and having a long snout and claws for hunting ants and termites; native to Australia

<a id="entry-e-000271"></a>

*n.* 1. **蚤屬** — a genus of Siphonaptera

<a id="entry-e-000272"></a>

*n.* 1. **雞蚤** — parasitic on especially the heads of chickens

<a id="entry-e-000273"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000274"></a>
## echinoderm

**發音：** /ɪˈkaɪnə(ʊ)ˌdɜːm/
*n.* 1. **棘皮動物 /棘皮動物** — An animal of the phylum Echinodermata, comprising radially symmetric, spiny-skinned marine animals including seastars, sea urchins, sea cucumbers, crinoids, and sand dollars.
2. **棘皮動物** — marine invertebrates with tube feet and five-part radially symmetrical bodies

<a id="entry-e-000275"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000279"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000280"></a>

*n.* 1. **海膽** — a sea urchin that can be eaten

<a id="entry-e-000281"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000282"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000283"></a>
## Echo

**發音：** /ˈɛkoʊ/
*prop. n.* 1. **司音星** — 60 Echo, a main belt asteroid.

<a id="entry-e-000284"></a>
## echo

**發音：** /ˈɛk.əʊ/
*n.* 1. **反響, 迴響, 回聲, 迴音** — the repetition of a sound resulting from reflection of the sound waves
*“she could hear echoes of her own footsteps”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **回聲 /回聲, 應聲 /應聲** — A reflected sound that is heard again by its initial observer.
3. (Greek mythology) a nymph who was spurned by Narcissus and pined away until only her voice remained
4. a reply that repeats what has just been said
5. a reflected television or radio or radar beam
6. a close parallel of a feeling, idea, style, etc.
*“his contention contains more than an echo of Rousseau”*
*“Napoleon III was an echo of the mighty Emperor but an infinitely better man”*
*v.* 1. **反響, 迴響, 使迴響, 鳴響** — ring or echo with sound
*“the hall resounded with laughter”*
2. **召回, 回憶, 回想** — call to mind
*“His words echoed John F. Kennedy”*
3. to say again or imitate
*“followers echoing the cries of their leaders”*

<a id="entry-e-000285"></a>
## echo chamber

*n.* 1. **迴音室, 混響室 /混響室** — A room or other enclosed space that is highly conducive to the production of echoes, particularly one that has been designed and built for this purpose.
2. **回聲效果 /回聲效果, 混響效果 /混響效果** — A sound effect that may be applied to live or recorded sounds through a sound editing process, which creates the impression that the sounds originated in an enclosed space which was conducive to producing echoes or echoing.
3. *(broadly, figuratively)* **信息繭房 /信息繭房** — An environment in which a person is exposed to only those beliefs and opinions that agree with their own, so that their existing views are magnified and reinforced while alternative ideas are not expressed or considered.
4. an enclosed space for producing reverberation of a sound

<a id="entry-e-000286"></a>
## echocardiograph

*n.* 1. **超聲心動圖儀 /超聲心動圖儀** — A device that uses ultrasound to produce images of the heart.
2. **心回波儀, 超聲波心動儀** — a sonograph that creates an image of the heart and its abnormalities

<a id="entry-e-000287"></a>
## echoencephalograph

*n.* 1. **回波腦造影儀, 腦回波圖儀** — a sonograph that creates an image of the brain and its abnormalities

<a id="entry-e-000288"></a>
## echoic

*adj.* 1. **像回聲+的, 迴響似+的, 有回聲性質+的, 回聲+的** — like or characteristic of an echo
2. (of words) formed in imitation of a natural sound
*“onomatopoeic words are imitative of noises”*
*“it was independently developed in more than one place as an onomatopoetic term"- Harry Hoijer”*

<a id="entry-e-000289"></a>
## echoing

*adj.* 1. **回聲+的** — (of sounds) repeating by reflection
*“a hotel with echoing halls”*

<a id="entry-e-000290"></a>
## echolalia

**發音：** /ˌɛkə(ʊ)ˈleɪlɪə/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **模仿言語 /模仿言語** — The involuntary repetitive echoing of words or phrases spoken by another person; either immediate or delayed.
2. an infant's repetition of sounds uttered by others
3. (psychiatry) mechanical and meaningless repetition of the words of another person (as in schizophrenia)

<a id="entry-e-000291"></a>
## echoless

*adj.* 1. **無回聲+的** — having or producing no echo
*“the echoless darkness”*

<a id="entry-e-000292"></a>
## echolike

*adj.* 1. **像回聲+的, 迴響似+的, 有回聲性質+的, 回聲+的** — like or characteristic of an echo

<a id="entry-e-000293"></a>
## echolocation

**發音：** /ˌɛkoʊloʊˈkeɪʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **回聲定位 /回聲定位** — The use of echoes to detect objects as observed in bats and other natural creatures.
2. determining the location of something by measuring the time it takes for an echo to return from it

<a id="entry-e-000294"></a>
## echt

*adj.* 1. **名副其實+的, 真正+的** — not fake or counterfeit
*“a genuine Picasso”*
*“genuine leather”*

<a id="entry-e-000295"></a>
## Eckankar

*prop. n.* 1. **埃坎卡** — A pantheistic religion founded in 1965 by Paul Twitchell.

<a id="entry-e-000296"></a>
## eclampsia

**發音：** /ɪˈklæmp.si.ə/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **子癇 /子癇** — A complication of pregnancy characterized by seizures and coma due to hypertension.
2. **子癇驚厥, 驚厥** — a toxic condition characterized by convulsions and possibly coma during or immediately after pregnancy

<a id="entry-e-000297"></a>
## eclat

*n.* 1. **喝彩, 拍手喝采, 歡呼, 讚美, 稱讚** — enthusiastic approval
*“the book met with modest acclaim”*
*“he acknowledged the plaudits of the crowd”*
2. ceremonial elegance and splendor
*“entered with much eclat in a coach drawn by eight white horses”*
3. brilliant or conspicuous success or effect
*“the eclat of a great achievement”*

<a id="entry-e-000298"></a>
## eclecticism

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **折中主義 /折中主義** — Any form of art that borrows from multiple other styles.
2. making decisions on the basis of what seems best instead of following some single doctrine or style

<a id="entry-e-000299"></a>
## eclipse

**發音：** /ɪˈklɪps/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **日食, 日蝕 /日蝕, 月食, 月蝕 /月蝕, 交食** — Especially, an alignment whereby a planetary object (for example, the Moon) comes between the Sun and another planetary object (for example, the Earth), resulting in a shadow being cast by the middle planetary object onto the other planetary object.
2. one celestial body obscures another
*v.* 1. **使黯然失色, 使失色, 使陰暗** — be greater in significance than
*“the tragedy overshadowed the couple's happiness”*
2. **食, 掩蔽, 遮掩** — cause an eclipse of (a celestial body) by intervention
*“The Sun eclipses the moon today”*
*“Planets and stars often are occulted by other celestial bodies”*

<a id="entry-e-000300"></a>
## eclipsis

*n.* 1. **省略** — omission or suppression of parts of words or sentences

<a id="entry-e-000301"></a>
## eclipta

**發音：** /ɛˈklɪp.tə/
*n.* 1. **鱧腸 /鱧腸** — Any asteraceous flowering plant of the genus Eclipta.

<a id="entry-e-000302"></a>
## ecliptic

**發音：** /ɪˈklɪp.tɪk/
*n.* 1. **黃道 /黃道** — The great circle on the celestial sphere that is the mean apparent path of the sun as viewed from the earth.
2. the great circle representing the apparent annual path of the sun; the plane of the Earth's orbit around the sun; makes an angle of about 23 degrees with the equator
*“all of the planets rotate the sun in approximately the same ecliptic”*

<a id="entry-e-000303"></a>
## eclogite

**發音：** /ˈɛklədʒʌɪt/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **榴輝巖 /榴輝巖** — A coarse-grained metamorphic rock, a mixture of pyroxene, quartz, and feldspar with inclusions of red garnet.

<a id="entry-e-000304"></a>
## eco-

**發音：** /ˈiːkəʊ/
*pref.* 1. *(morpheme)* **生** — ecology or the environment (in the ecological sense)

<a id="entry-e-000305"></a>
## eco-city

*n.* 1. **生態城市 /生態城市** — A city which is regarded as having sound environmental protection policies.

<a id="entry-e-000306"></a>
## eco-region

*n.* 1. **生態區域 /生態區域** — A region, smaller than an ecozone, that contains a distinct biodiversity of flora and fauna.

<a id="entry-e-000307"></a>
## ecocide

**發音：** /ˈiːkəʊsaɪd/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **生態滅絕 /生態滅絕, 生態滅絕罪 /生態滅絕罪** — The complete destruction of an ecosystem due to human activities, such as due to exploitation of resources, nuclear warfare, or the dumping of harmful chemicals.

<a id="entry-e-000308"></a>
## ecoduct

**發音：** /ˈiːkəʊdʌkt/
*n.* 1. **生態廊道 /生態廊道** — A type of wildlife crossing in the form of a bridge or tunnel, usually covered with foliage and turf, which animals may use to cross roads without the risk of being hit by vehicles; a wildlife viaduct.

<a id="entry-e-000309"></a>
## ecofeminism

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **生態女性主義 /生態女性主義** — A sociopolitical movement combining feminism and environmentalism.

<a id="entry-e-000310"></a>
## ecologic

*adj.* 1. **生態學+的, 生態+的** — characterized by the interdependence of living organisms in an environment
*“an ecological disaster”*
2. **生態+的, 生態學+的** — of or relating to the science of ecology
*“ecological research”*

<a id="entry-e-000311"></a>
## ecological

**發音：** /ˌiːkəˈlɒd͡ʒɪkl̩/
*adj.* 1. **生態的 /生態的, 生態學的 /生態學的** — Relating to ecology, the interrelationships of organisms and their environment.
2. **生態學+的, 生態+的** — characterized by the interdependence of living organisms in an environment
*“an ecological disaster”*
3. **生態+的, 生態學+的** — of or relating to the science of ecology
*“ecological research”*

<a id="entry-e-000312"></a>
## ecological footprint

*n.* 1. **生態足跡 /生態足跡** — A measure of how much biologically productive land and water area an individual, population or activity requires to produce all the resources it consumes and to absorb the waste it generates using prevailing technology and resource management practices.

<a id="entry-e-000313"></a>
## ecological niche

*n.* 1. **功能, 生態小生境, 生態龕位** — (ecology) the status of an organism within its environment and community (affecting its survival as a species)

<a id="entry-e-000314"></a>
## ecological succession

*n.* 1. **演替, 自然演替** — (ecology) the gradual and orderly process of change in an ecosystem brought about by the progressive replacement of one community by another until a stable climax is established

<a id="entry-e-000315"></a>
## ecologism

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **生態主義 /生態主義** — An ideology focusing on ecology and the environment.

<a id="entry-e-000316"></a>
## ecologist

**發音：** /iːˈkɒləd͡ʒɪst/
*n.* 1. **生態學家 /生態學家** — A scholar of ecology.
2. a biologist who studies the relation between organisms and their environment

<a id="entry-e-000317"></a>
## ecology

**發音：** /ɪˈkɒl.ə.d͡ʒi/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **生態 /生態, 生態學 /生態學** — The branch of biology dealing with the relationships of organisms with their environment and with each other.
2. **環境保護學, 生態學** — the environment as it relates to living organisms
*“it changed the ecology of the island”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **生態 /生態** — The totality or pattern of relationships of components of a system with their environment and with each other.
4. the branch of biology concerned with the relations between organisms and their environment

<a id="entry-e-000318"></a>
## econometric

*adj.* 1. **計量經濟學+的** — of or relating to econometrics
*“econometric theories”*

<a id="entry-e-000319"></a>
## econometrics

**發音：** /ɪˌkɒn.əˈmɛt.ɹɪks/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **計量經濟學 /計量經濟學** — The branch of economics that applies statistical methods to the empirical study of economic theories and relationships.
2. the application of mathematics and statistics to the study of economic and financial data

<a id="entry-e-000320"></a>
## economic

**發音：** /ˌiːkəˈnɒmɪk/
*adj.* 1. **經濟+的, 經濟上+的** — of or relating to an economy, the system of production and management of material wealth
*“economic growth”*
*“aspects of social, political, and economical life”*
2. **經濟學+的** — of or relating to the science of economics
*“economic theory”*
3. **經濟 /經濟** — Pertaining to an economy.
4. **合算+的, 有利可圖+的, 經濟上+的** — concerned with worldly necessities of life (especially money)
*“he wrote the book primarily for economic reasons”*
*“gave up the large house for economic reasons”*
5. using the minimum of time or resources necessary for effectiveness
*“an economic use of home heating oil”*
*“a modern economical heating system”*
6. financially rewarding
*“it was no longer economic to keep the factory open”*
*“have to keep prices high enough to make it economic to continue the service”*

<a id="entry-e-000321"></a>
## economic aid

*n.* 1. **經濟援助, 資助, 獎金** — money to support a worthy person or cause

<a id="entry-e-000322"></a>
## economic consumption

*n.* 1. **消費** — (economics) the utilization of economic goods to satisfy needs or in manufacturing
*“the consumption of energy has increased steadily”*

<a id="entry-e-000323"></a>
## economic crisis

*n.* 1. **經濟危機 /經濟危機** — A crisis caused by the general demand for goods within an economy falling below their supply, either temporarily or on a protracted basis, typically resulting in widespread bankruptcies of unproductive businesses and falling prices.
2. **經濟危機, 蕭條, 衰退** — a long-term economic state characterized by unemployment and low prices and low levels of trade and investment

<a id="entry-e-000324"></a>
## economic expert

*n.* 1. **經濟學家** — an expert in the science of economics

<a id="entry-e-000325"></a>
## economic growth

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **經濟增長 /經濟增長** — The growth of the economic output of a country.
2. **經濟增長** — steady growth in the productive capacity of the economy (and so a growth of national income)

<a id="entry-e-000326"></a>
## economic liberalism

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **經濟自由主義 /經濟自由主義** — The ideological belief in organising the economy on individual lines in such a way as to have the greatest possible number of economic decisions made by individuals or households and not by collective institutions or organisations.

<a id="entry-e-000327"></a>
## economic migrant

*n.* 1. **經濟移民 /經濟移民** — A person who emigrates from one region to another to seek an improvement in living standards.

<a id="entry-e-000328"></a>
## economic process

*n.* 1. **經濟活動, 經濟過程** — any process affecting the production and development and management of material wealth

<a id="entry-e-000329"></a>
## economic rent

*n.* 1. **經濟地租, 經濟租金** — the return derived from cultivated land in excess of that derived from the poorest land cultivated under similar conditions

<a id="entry-e-000330"></a>
## economic science

*n.* 1. **經濟學** — the branch of social science that deals with the production and distribution and consumption of goods and services and their management

<a id="entry-e-000331"></a>
## economic system

*n.* 1. **經濟, 經濟體系** — the system of production and distribution and consumption

<a id="entry-e-000332"></a>
## economic value

*n.* 1. **估價, 評價, 價值** — the amount (of money or goods or services) that is considered to be a fair equivalent for something else
*“he tried to estimate the value of the produce at normal prices”*

<a id="entry-e-000333"></a>
## economic warfare

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **經濟戰 /經濟戰, 經濟戰** — The strategic use of economic measures (such as sanctions, embargoes, tariffs, or the freezing of assets) to weaken the economy of another state, often to achieve political or military objectives.

<a id="entry-e-000334"></a>
## economical

**發音：** /ˌiː.kəˈnɒm.ɪ.kəl/
*adj.* 1. **經濟+的, 經濟上+的** — of or relating to an economy, the system of production and management of material wealth
*“economic growth”*
*“aspects of social, political, and economical life”*
2. **節儉+的** — avoiding waste
*“an economical meal”*
*“an economical shopper”*
3. **節約的 /節約的** — Careful with money so as not to spend too much; prudent; thrifty.
4. **經濟實惠的 /經濟實惠的** — Saving money or resources.
5. using the minimum of time or resources necessary for effectiveness
*“an economic use of home heating oil”*
*“a modern economical heating system”*

<a id="entry-e-000335"></a>
## economics

**發音：** /ˌiːkəˈnɒmɪks/
*n.* 1. **經濟學** — the branch of social science that deals with the production and distribution and consumption of goods and services and their management
2. *(dated, plural, singular)* **經濟學 /經濟學** — Now chiefly home economics: the art or science of household management, especially relating to the appropriate organization of resources; housekeeping.

<a id="entry-e-000336"></a>
## economies of scale

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-normally)* **規模經濟 /規模經濟, 規模效益 /規模效益** — The characteristics of a production process in which an increase in the scale of the firm causes a decrease in the long run average cost of each unit.

<a id="entry-e-000337"></a>
## economise

*v.* 1. **有效地利用, 節約, 節省** — spend sparingly, avoid the waste of
*“This move will save money”*
*“The less fortunate will have to economize now”*
2. **節儉, 節省, 節約** — use cautiously and frugally
*“I try to economize my spare time”*
*“conserve your energy for the ascent to the summit”*

<a id="entry-e-000338"></a>
## economist

**發音：** /ɪˈkɒn.ə.mɪst/
*n.* 1. **經濟學家** — an expert in the science of economics
2. **經濟學家 /經濟學家** — An expert in economics, especially one who studies economic data and extracts higher-level information or proposes theories.

<a id="entry-e-000339"></a>
## economize

**發音：** /iˈkɑnəmaɪz/
*v.* 1. **節儉, 節省, 節約** — use cautiously and frugally
*“I try to economize my spare time”*
*“conserve your energy for the ascent to the summit”*
2. *(transitive)* **節約 /節約, 節省 /節省, 撙節 /撙節** — To use frugally.
3. **有效地利用, 節約, 節省** — spend sparingly, avoid the waste of
*“This move will save money”*
*“The less fortunate will have to economize now”*

<a id="entry-e-000340"></a>
## economy

**發音：** /ɪˈkɒ.nə.mi/
*n.* 1. **經濟, 經濟體系** — the system of production and distribution and consumption
2. **節儉, 節約** — frugality in the expenditure of money or resources
*“the Scots are famous for their economy”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **經濟 /經濟** — The system of production and distribution and consumption. The overall measure of a currency system; as the national economy.
4. the efficient use of resources
*“economy of effort”*
5. an act of economizing; reduction in cost
*“it was a small economy to walk to work every day”*
*“there was a saving of 50 cents”*

<a id="entry-e-000341"></a>
## economy class

*n.* 1. **二等艙** — a class of accommodations on a ship or train or plane that are less expensive than first class accommodations

<a id="entry-e-000342"></a>
## ecosystem

**發音：** /ˈiːkəʊˌsɪstəm/
*n.* 1. **生態系 /生態系, 生態系統 /生態系統** — A system formed by an ecological community and its environment that functions as a unit.
2. a system formed by the interaction of a community of organisms with their physical environment

<a id="entry-e-000343"></a>
## ecoterrorism

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **生態恐怖主義 /生態恐怖主義** — Terrorism with an ecological motive, such as attacks against corporations perceived to be harming the natural environment.
2. violence carried out to further the political or social objectives of the environmentalists

<a id="entry-e-000344"></a>
## ecoterrorist

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **生態旅遊 /生態旅遊, 生態觀光 /生態觀光** — Responsible (sustainable) travel to natural areas supporting the fauna, flora, and local economy.
2. tourism to exotic or threatened ecosystems to observe wildlife or to help preserve nature

## edge

**發音：** /ɛd͡ʒ/
*n.* 1. **分界線, 界限, 邊界, 範圍** — a line determining the limits of an area
2. **邊緣** — a sharp side formed by the intersection of two surfaces of an object
*“he rounded the edges of the box”*
3. **小優勢** — a slight competitive advantage
*“he had an edge on the competition”*
4. **邊緣** — the outside limit of an object or area or surface; a place farthest away from the center of something
*“the edge of the leaf is wavy”*
*“she sat on the edge of the bed”*
5. **邊界 /邊界, 邊緣 /邊緣** — The boundary line of a surface.
6. *(also, figuratively)* **刃** — The thin cutting side of the blade of an instrument, such as an ax, knife, sword, or scythe; that which cuts as an edge does, or wounds deeply, etc.
*v.* 1. **徐徐移動, 徐進, 慢慢前進, 漸漸地移動, 緩慢地移動** — advance slowly, as if by inches
*“He edged towards the car”*
2. *(intransitive, slang, transitive)* **寸止** — To intentionally stay or keep someone extremely close to the point of orgasm for a long period of time.
3. provide with a border or edge
*“edge the tablecloth with embroidery”*
4. lie adjacent to another or share a boundary
*“Canada adjoins the U.S.”*
*“England marches with Scotland”*
5. provide with an edge
*“edge a blade”*

## efface

*“efface the memory of the time in the camps”*

*“Please erase the formula on the blackboard--it is wrong!”*

<a id="entry-e-000459"></a>
## effect

**發音：** /ɪˈfɛkt/
*n.* 1. **後果, 效果, 結果, 影響** — a phenomenon that follows and is caused by some previous phenomenon
*“the magnetic effect was greater when the rod was lengthwise”*
*“his decision had depressing consequences for business”*
2. **印象** — an outward appearance
*“he made a good impression”*
*“I wanted to create an impression of success”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **效果, 影響 /影響, 結果 /結果** — The result or outcome of a cause.
4. **效果, 影響** — a symptom caused by an illness or a drug
*“the effects of sleep loss”*
*“the effect of the anesthetic”*
5. an impression (especially one that is artificial or contrived)
*“he just did it for effect”*
6. the central meaning or theme of a speech or literary work
*v.* 1. **使發生, 使存在, 引起** — act so as to bring into existence
*“effect a change”*
2. produce
*“The scientists set up a shock wave”*

<a id="entry-e-000460"></a>
## effected

*adj.* 1. **已確認+的, 已經制定+的, 已被確認+的** — settled securely and unconditionally
*“that smoking causes health problems is an accomplished fact”*

<a id="entry-e-000461"></a>
## effective

*adj.* 1. **有效果+的, 有力+的, 有效+的** — producing or capable of producing an intended result or having a striking effect
*“an air-cooled motor was more effective than a witch's broomstick for rapid long-distance transportation"-LewisMumford”*
*“effective teaching methods”*
2. able to accomplish a purpose; functioning effectively
*“people who will do nothing unless they get something out of it for themselves are often highly effective persons..."-G.B.Shaw”*
*“effective personnel”*
3. works well as a means or remedy
*“an effective reprimand”*
*“a lotion that is effective in cases of prickly heat”*
4. exerting force or influence
*“the law is effective immediately”*
*“a warranty good for two years”*
5. existing in fact; not theoretical; real
*“a decline in the effective demand”*
*“confused increased equipment and expenditure with the quantity of effective work done”*
6. ready for service
*“the fort was held by about 100 effective soldiers”*

## egg roll

*n.* 1. **潤餅 /潤餅** — Chinese-American variant of the spring roll (春捲); food made made by wrapping a combination of chopped vegetables, possibly meat, and sometimes noodles, in a sheet of dough, dipping the dough in egg or an egg wash, then deep frying it.
2. *(Malaysia, Singapore)* **雞蛋卷 /雞蛋卷, 蛋卷** — A thin crispy biscuit roll similar to the Spanish barquillo, Dutch kniepertjes, and Norwegian krumkake. Usually made from coconut milk, eggs, flour, sugar, and oil. This food item has many names: in Malay, kuih/kueh/kue kapit/sepit/sapit/semprong (literally "clamped/tongs/roll cake/snack"); in Chinese, 蛋卷 (literally "egg roll"); also often called "love letter" in Singapore and Malaysia (probably arising from the flowery patterns, Chinese characters, animals, etc. that were probably introduced by Peranakans). Usually eaten only around festivals such as Chinese New Year or Hari Raya Puasa. Sometimes also folded (rather than rolled). Note: while "egg roll" is a name sometimes used for this food item in Singapore and Malaysia, in the West, the name "egg roll" usually refers to a very different, savoury Chinese-American food item that is usually served hot and has little or no egg.
3. minced vegetables and meat wrapped in a pancake and fried

<a id="entry-e-000503"></a>

## eigenfunction

<a id="entry-e-000573"></a>
## eigenspace

<a id="entry-e-000574"></a>
## eigenvalue

**發音：** /ˈaɪ.ɡənˌvæl.juː/
*n.* 1. **特徵值 /特徵值, 本徵值 /本徵值** — A scalar λ, such that there exists a non-zero vector x (a corresponding eigenvector) for which the image of x under a given linear transformation A is equal to the image of x under multiplication by λ; i.e. Ax=λx.
2. (mathematics) any number such that a given square matrix minus that number times the identity matrix has a zero determinant

<a id="entry-e-000575"></a>
## eigenvector

*n.* 1. **本徵向量 /本徵向量, 特徵向量 /特徵向量** — A vector that is only scaled (not rotated out of its span) under a particular linear transformation; a left or right eigenvector depending on context; (more formally) given a linear transformation A, a vector x such that Ax=λx [or xA=λx] for some scalar λ (called the eigenvalue).

*“a detailed plan”*

*v.* 1. **詳細地說明, 詳細說明, 詳細闡述, 變得複雜, 擴充** — add details, as to an account or idea; clarify the meaning of and discourse in a learned way, usually in writing

*“refine a design or pattern”*

4. work out in detail

6. any of numerous shrubs or small trees of temperate and subtropical northern hemisphere having white flowers and berrylike fruit

*“We elected him chairman of the board”*
2. **選擇, 選舉** — choose
*“I elected to have my funds deposited automatically”*
3. *(transitive)* **選擇 /選擇, 選舉 /選舉** — To choose or make a decision (to do something).

*“the results of the election will be announced tonight”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **選舉 /選舉** — A process of choosing a leader, members of parliament, councillors, or other representatives by popular vote.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **選任 /選任, 選出 /選出** — The choice of a leader or representative by popular vote.

<a id="entry-e-000776"></a>
## electrical contact

*n.* 1. **電氣插頭** — contact that allows current to pass from one conductor to another

<a id="entry-e-000777"></a>
## electrical converter

*n.* 1. **電轉換器** — converter that converts alternating current into direct current or vice versa

<a id="entry-e-000778"></a>
## electrical device

*n.* 1. **電力設備** — a device that produces or is powered by electricity

<a id="entry-e-000779"></a>
## electrical distributor

*n.* 1. **配電盤** — electrical device that distributes voltage to the spark plugs of a gasoline engine in the order of the firing sequence

<a id="entry-e-000780"></a>
## electrical energy

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電能 /電能, 電力 /電力** — Energy made available for consumption or consumed in the form of electricity or electric power; electricity.
2. energy made available by the flow of electric charge through a conductor
*“they built a car that runs on electricity”*

<a id="entry-e-000781"></a>
## electrical engineering

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電機工程 /電機工程** — The branch of engineering that deals with the technology of electricity, especially the design and application of circuitry and equipment for power generation and distribution, machine control, and communications.
2. the branch of engineering science that studies the uses of electricity and the equipment for power generation and distribution and the control of machines and communication

<a id="entry-e-000782"></a>
## electrical fuse

*n.* 1. **保險絲** — an electrical device that can interrupt the flow of electrical current when it is overloaded

<a id="entry-e-000783"></a>
## electrical outlet

*n.* 1. **牆上插頭, 插孔, 插座** — receptacle providing a place in a wiring system where current can be taken to run electrical devices

<a id="entry-e-000784"></a>
## electrical plant

*n.* 1. **電器廠** — utility that provides electricity

<a id="entry-e-000785"></a>
## electrical resistance

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **電阻 /電阻** — The opposition offered by an electrical conductor to the flow of a current through itself, resulting in a conversion of electrical energy into heat and radiation. The SI derived unit of resistance is the ohm (Ω). Symbol: R.
2. a material's opposition to the flow of electric current; measured in ohms

<a id="entry-e-000786"></a>
## electrical shock

*n.* 1. **電擊** — a reflex response to the passage of electric current through the body
*“subjects received a small electric shock when they made the wrong response”*
*“electricians get accustomed to occasional shocks”*

<a id="entry-e-000787"></a>
## electrical switch

*n.* 1. **開關** — control consisting of a mechanical or electrical or electronic device for making or breaking or changing the connections in a circuit

<a id="entry-e-000788"></a>
## electrical system

*n.* 1. **電器廠** — utility that provides electricity
2. **電氣系統** — equipment in a motor vehicle that provides electricity to start the engine and ignite the fuel and operate the lights and windshield wiper and heater and air conditioner and radio

<a id="entry-e-000789"></a>
## electrical tape

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **絕緣膠帶 /絕緣膠帶** — A poorly conductive plastic tape used to insulate electrical wires.

<a id="entry-e-000790"></a>
## electrical wiring

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **電力線路 /電力線路** — The implementation of electrical wires and related equipment as a planned step in and a result of the finalization of a building.

<a id="entry-e-000791"></a>
## electrician

**發音：** /ɪˌlɛkˈtɹɪʃ(ə)n/
*n.* 1. **電工 /電工** — A tradesperson who installs, repairs and maintains electrical wiring and equipment.
2. a person who installs or repairs electrical or telephone lines

<a id="entry-e-000792"></a>
## electricity

**發音：** /ˌiːlɛkˈtɹɪsɪti/
*n.* 1. **電** — a physical phenomenon associated with stationary or moving electrons and protons
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **電力 /電力** — Electrical power, as supplied by power stations or generators.
3. *(uncountable, usually)* **電 /電, 電氣 /電氣, 電能 /電能, 電力 /電力** — Electrical energy, as supplied by power stations or generators.
4. energy made available by the flow of electric charge through a conductor
*“they built a car that runs on electricity”*
5. keen and shared excitement
*“the stage crackled with electricity whenever she was on it”*

<a id="entry-e-000793"></a>
## electrification

**發音：** /ɪˈlɛktɹɪfɪkeɪʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **電氣化 /電氣化, 電化 /電化, 帶電 /帶電** — The act of electrifying, or the state of being charged with electricity.
2. the activity of thrilling or markedly exciting some person or group
3. the act of providing electricity
*“the electrification of rural Tennessee”*

<a id="entry-e-000794"></a>
## electrify

**發音：** /ɪˈlɛktɹɪfaɪ/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **電氣化 /電氣化** — To adapt (a home, farm, village, city, industry, vehicle, railroad) for electric power.
2. excite suddenly and intensely
*“The news electrified us”*
3. charge (a conductor) with electricity
4. equip for use with electricity
*“electrify an appliance”*

<a id="entry-e-000795"></a>
## electro-

*pref.* 1. *(form-of, in-compounds, morpheme)* **電 /電** — Combining form of electricity.

<a id="entry-e-000796"></a>
## electro-acoustic transducer

*n.* 1. **電聲傳感器** — a transducer that converts electrical to acoustic energy or vice versa

<a id="entry-e-000797"></a>
## electrobiology

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電生物學** — The study of the production and use of electricity by biological organisms.

<a id="entry-e-000798"></a>
## electrocardiogram

**發音：** /əˌlɛktɹəˈkɑɹdi.əˌɡɹæm/
*n.* 1. **心電圖 /心電圖** — The recording that an electrocardiograph produces.
2. a graphical recording of the cardiac cycle produced by an electrocardiograph

<a id="entry-e-000799"></a>
## electrocardiograph

*n.* 1. **心動描記器, 心動電描記器, 心電描記器** — medical instrument that records electric currents associated with contractions of the heart
2. **心電圖儀 /心電圖儀** — A device used in the diagnosis and detection of heart abnormalities that measures electric potentials on the surface of the body and creates a record (electrocardiogram) of the electrical currents associated with heart muscle activity.

<a id="entry-e-000800"></a>
## electrocardiographic

*adj.* 1. **心電圖儀+的** — of or relating to an electrocardiograph

<a id="entry-e-000801"></a>
## electrocardiography

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **心電描記術 /心電描記術, 心電圖學 /心電圖學** — The science and practice of the creation and diagnostic interpretation of electrocardiograms.
2. diagnostic procedure consisting of recording the activity of the heart electronically with a cardiograph (and producing a cardiogram)

<a id="entry-e-000802"></a>
## electrochemical

*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **電化學 /電化學** — Of, or relating to a chemical reaction brought about by electricity.
2. **電化學+的, 電氣化學+的** — of or involving electrochemistry
3. *(not-comparable)* **電化學 /電化學** — Of, or relating to electricity produced by a chemical reaction, e.g. in a battery.

<a id="entry-e-000803"></a>
## electrochemistry

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **電化學 /電化學** — The science of the chemistry associated with the flow of electricity, especially at the surface of an electrode.
2. branch of chemistry that deals with the chemical action of electricity and the production of electricity by chemical reactions

<a id="entry-e-000804"></a>
## electroconvulsive therapy

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **電痙攣療法 /電痙攣療法, 電休克療法 /電休克療法, 電抽搐治療 /電抽搐治療, 電驚厥療法 /電驚厥療法** — A biomedical therapy for severely depressed patients in which a brief electric current is sent through the brain of an anesthetized patient, with the noted side effect of memory loss.
2. the administration of a strong electric current that passes through the brain to induce convulsions and coma

<a id="entry-e-000805"></a>
## electrocute

**發音：** /ɪˈlɛktɹəkjuːt/
*v.* 1. **以電擊殺死** — kill by electric shock
*“She dropped the hair dryer into the bathtub and was instantly electrocuted”*
2. **以電擊殺死, 施以電刑, 通電致死** — kill by electrocution, as in the electric chair
*“The serial killer was electrocuted”*
3. *(informal, proscribed, transitive)* **電擊 /電擊** — To inflict a severe electric shock (not necessarily fatal) upon.

<a id="entry-e-000806"></a>
## electrocution

**發音：** /ɪˌlɛktɹəˈkjuːʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **電刑 /電刑** — Deliberate execution by electric shock, usually involving an electric chair.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **電死 /電死** — The accidental death or suicide by electric shock.
3. *(countable, informal, proscribed)* **觸電 /觸電** — A severe electric shock, whether fatal or not.
4. execution by electricity
5. killing by electric shock

<a id="entry-e-000807"></a>
## electrode

**發音：** /əˈlɛk.tɹəʊd/
*n.* 1. **發射極, 電極, 集電極** — a conductor used to make electrical contact with some part of a circuit
2. **電極 /電極** — The terminal through which electric current passes between metallic and nonmetallic parts of an electric circuit.

<a id="entry-e-000808"></a>
## electrodeposition

*n.* 1. **電極澱積, 電沉積** — the deposition of a substance on an electrode by the action of electricity (especially by electrolysis)

<a id="entry-e-000809"></a>
## electrodynamometer

*n.* 1. **力測電流計, 電力測功計, 電功率計, 電測力計** — measuring instrument that uses the interaction of the magnetic fields of two coils to measure current or voltage or power

<a id="entry-e-000810"></a>
## electroencephalogram

**發音：** /əˌlɛktɹoʊ.ənˈsɛfələˌɡɹæm/
*n.* 1. **腦電圖 /腦電圖** — A recording of electrical brain activity (brain waves) made by an electroencephalograph.
2. a graphical record of electrical activity of the brain; produced by an electroencephalograph

<a id="entry-e-000811"></a>
## electroencephalograph

*n.* 1. **電腦波測量圖, 腦電圖儀, 腦電描記器** — medical instrument that records electric currents generated by the brain

<a id="entry-e-000812"></a>
## electroencephalographic

*adj.* 1. **腦電圖儀+的, 腦電描記器+的** — of or relating to an electroencephalograph

<a id="entry-e-000813"></a>
## electroencephalography

**發音：** /əlɛktɹəʊɛnsɛfəˈlɒɡɹəfi/
*n.* 1. **腦電描記法 /腦電描記法** — The measurement and recording of electrical activity in the brain for diagnostic purposes.

<a id="entry-e-000814"></a>
## electrograph

*n.* 1. **傳真機** — an apparatus for the electrical transmission of pictures
2. **電刻器, 電雕刻器** — electrical device used for etching by electrolytic means

<a id="entry-e-000815"></a>
## electrokinetics

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **動電學 /動電學** — the electrodynamics of electrical circuits

<a id="entry-e-000816"></a>
## electrolarynx

*n.* 1. **電子喉 /電子喉** — A type of mechanical larynx to assist those who have lost their original voicebox to produce clearer speech.

<a id="entry-e-000817"></a>
## electrolysis

**發音：** /ɪlɛkˈtɹɒləsɪs/
*n.* 1. **電解** — (chemistry) a chemical decomposition reaction produced by passing an electric current through a solution containing ions
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **電解 /電解** — The chemical change produced by passing an electric current through a conducting solution or a molten salt.
3. removing superfluous or unwanted hair by passing an electric current through the hair root

<a id="entry-e-000818"></a>
## electrolyte

**發音：** /ɪˈlɛk.tɹəˌlaɪt/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **電解質 /電解質** — A substance that, in solution or when molten, ionizes and conducts electricity.
2. **電解液, 電解質** — a solution that conducts electricity
*“the proper amount and distribution of electrolytes in the body is essential for health”*

<a id="entry-e-000819"></a>
## electrolyte acid

*n.* 1. **電瓶水, 蓄電池用酸, 蓄電池酸液** — dilute sulfuric acid used in storage batteries

<a id="entry-e-000820"></a>
## electrolyte balance

*n.* 1. **電解質平衡** — an equilibrium between the amounts of electrolytes (as calcium and sodium and potassium) that is essential for normal health and functioning

<a id="entry-e-000821"></a>
## electrolytic

*n.* 1. **電解質電容器** — a fixed capacitor consisting of two electrodes separated by an electrolyte
*adj.* 1. **由電解產生, 電解+的** — of or concerned with or produced by electrolysis
2. **電解+的** — of or relating to or containing an electrolyte

<a id="entry-e-000822"></a>
## electrolytic capacitor

*n.* 1. **電解電容 /電解電容** — A capacitor that uses an electrolyte as one of its capacitative plates.
2. **電解質電容器** — a fixed capacitor consisting of two electrodes separated by an electrolyte

<a id="entry-e-000823"></a>
## electrolytic cell

*n.* 1. **電解電池** — a cell containing an electrolyte in which an applied voltage causes a reaction to occur that would not occur otherwise (such as the breakdown of water into hydrogen and oxygen)

<a id="entry-e-000824"></a>
## electrolytic condenser

*n.* 1. **電解質電容器** — a fixed capacitor consisting of two electrodes separated by an electrolyte

<a id="entry-e-000825"></a>
## electromagnet

*n.* 1. **電磁體, 電磁石** — a temporary magnet made by coiling wire around an iron core; when current flows in the coil the iron becomes a magnet
2. **電磁體 /電磁體, 電磁鐵 /電磁鐵** — A magnet which attracts metals only when electrically activated.

<a id="entry-e-000826"></a>
## electromagnetic

**發音：** /ɪˌlɛk.tɹoʊ.mæɡˈnɛ.tɪk/
*adj.* 1. **電磁+的** — pertaining to or exhibiting magnetism produced by electric charge in motion
*“electromagnetic energy”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **電磁 /電磁** — Relating to electromagnetism.

<a id="entry-e-000827"></a>
## electromagnetic delay line

*n.* 1. **電磁延遲線** — a delay line based on the time of propagation of electromagnetic waves

<a id="entry-e-000828"></a>
## electromagnetic energy

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **電磁能 /電磁能, 電磁能** — The potential energy of an electric or magnetic field.

<a id="entry-e-000829"></a>
## electromagnetic field

*n.* 1. **電磁場 /電磁場** — The field of force that is due to the interaction of electric and magnetic forces of charged bodies.

<a id="entry-e-000830"></a>
## electromagnetic force

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電磁力 /電磁力** — A long-range fundamental force that acts between charged bodies, mediated by the exchange of photons.

<a id="entry-e-000831"></a>
## electromagnetic induction

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **電磁感應 /電磁感應** — The production of an electrical potential difference in a conductor situated in a changing magnetic flux.

<a id="entry-e-000832"></a>
## electromagnetic interaction

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電磁相互作用 /電磁相互作用** — The fundamental interaction responsible for the electromagnetic force.
2. an interaction between charged elementary particles that is intermediate in strength between the strong and weak interactions; mediated by photons

<a id="entry-e-000833"></a>
## electromagnetic pulse

*n.* 1. **電磁脈衝 /電磁脈衝** — A short-duration burst of high-intensity electromagnetic radiation generated by a physical event, in particular by a large thermonuclear explosion in the atmosphere. The pulse causes a damaging surge of voltage within electrical and electronic systems.

<a id="entry-e-000834"></a>
## electromagnetic radiation

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電磁輻射 /電磁輻射** — Radiation (quantized as photons) consisting of oscillating electric and magnetic fields oriented perpendicularly to each other, moving through space.
2. radiation consisting of waves of energy associated with electric and magnetic fields resulting from the acceleration of an electric charge

<a id="entry-e-000835"></a>
## electromagnetic spectrum

*n.* 1. **電磁波譜 /電磁波譜** — The entire range of wavelengths of all known electromagnetic radiations extending from gamma rays, to X-rays through visible light, infrared, to radio waves.
2. the entire frequency range of electromagnetic waves

<a id="entry-e-000836"></a>
## electromagnetic unit

*n.* 1. **電磁單位** — any of various systems of units for measuring electricity and magnetism

<a id="entry-e-000837"></a>
## electromagnetic wave

*n.* 1. **電磁波 /電磁波** — Electromagnetic radiation, such as light and radio waves.
2. radiation consisting of waves of energy associated with electric and magnetic fields resulting from the acceleration of an electric charge

<a id="entry-e-000838"></a>
## electromagnetism

**發音：** /ɪˌlɛk.tɹəʊˈmæɡ.nəˌtɪzm̩/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **電磁學 /電磁學, 電磁 /電磁** — A unified fundamental force that combines the aspects of electricity and magnetism and is one of the four fundamental forces.
2. magnetism produced by an electric current
*“electromagnetism was discovered when it was observed that a copper wire carrying an electric current can magnetize pieces of iron or steel near it”*
3. the branch of physics concerned with electromagnetic phenomena

<a id="entry-e-000839"></a>
## electromechanical

*adj.* 1. **機電+的, 電動機械+的, 電機+的** — of or relating to or involving an electrically operated mechanical device

<a id="entry-e-000840"></a>
## electromechanical device

*n.* 1. **機電裝置** — a mechanical device that is operated by electricity

<a id="entry-e-000841"></a>
## electrometer

*n.* 1. **電位計, 量電錶, 靜電計** — meter to measure electrostatic voltage differences; draws no current from the source

<a id="entry-e-000842"></a>
## electromotive force

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **電動勢 /電動勢** — Potential energy (not force) divided by electric charge; measured in volts
2. the rate at which energy is drawn from a source that produces a flow of electricity in a circuit; expressed in volts

<a id="entry-e-000843"></a>
## electromyogram

*n.* 1. **肌電圖 /肌電圖** — The record produced by an electromyograph in electromyography.
2. a graphical record of electric currents associated with muscle contractions

<a id="entry-e-000844"></a>
## electromyograph

*n.* 1. **肌電圖儀 /肌電圖儀** — A device used in electromyography to generate electromyograms.
2. **肌電描記器** — a medical instrument that records the electrical waves associated with the activity of skeletal muscles

<a id="entry-e-000845"></a>
## electron

**發音：** /ɪˈlɛk.tɹɒn/
*n.* 1. **電子** — an elementary particle with negative charge
2. *(particle)* **電子 /電子** — The subatomic particle having a negative charge and orbiting the nucleus; the flow of electrons in a conductor constitutes electricity.

<a id="entry-e-000846"></a>
## electron accelerator

*n.* 1. **電子加速器** — collider that consists of an accelerator that collides electrons and positrons

<a id="entry-e-000847"></a>
## electron cloud

*n.* 1. **電子雲 /電子雲** — A mathematically defined region around an atomic nucleus defined by a high probability that the atom's electrons will be present in that region.

<a id="entry-e-000848"></a>
## electron density

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **電子密度 /電子密度** — A measure of the probability of finding an electron at a specific location within an orbital.

<a id="entry-e-000849"></a>
## electron gun

*n.* 1. **電子槍** — the electrode that is the source of electrons in a cathode-ray tube or electron microscope; consists of a cathode that emits a stream of electrons and the electrostatic or electromagnetic apparatus that focuses it

<a id="entry-e-000850"></a>
## electron lens

*n.* 1. **電子透鏡** — electronic equipment that uses a magnetic or electric field in order to focus a beam of electrons

<a id="entry-e-000851"></a>
## electron microscope

*n.* 1. **電子顯微鏡 /電子顯微鏡** — Any of several forms of microscope that use a beam of electrons, rather than one of light, and thus has a much greater resolving power.
2. **電子顯微鏡** — a microscope that is similar in purpose to a light microscope but achieves much greater resolving power by using a parallel beam of electrons to illuminate the object instead of a beam of light

<a id="entry-e-000852"></a>
## electron microscopic

*adj.* 1. **電子顯微鏡+的** — of or relating to or involving an electron microscope

<a id="entry-e-000853"></a>
## electron multiplier

*n.* 1. **電子倍增管** — a vacuum tube that amplifies a flow of electrons

<a id="entry-e-000854"></a>
## electron neutrino

*n.* 1. **電中微子** — A leptonic elementary particle, having near-zero mass and no charge

<a id="entry-e-000855"></a>
## electron tube

*n.* 1. **電子管 /電子管** — Any of several electronic devices that control electron current through vacuum, gas or vapor between electrodes within a sealed container, usually of glass or metal and often designed to be easily installed in and removed from the equipment in which it is used.
2. electronic device consisting of a system of electrodes arranged in an evacuated glass or metal envelope

<a id="entry-e-000856"></a>
## electron volt

**發音：** /ɪˈlek.trɑːn ˌvoʊlt/
*n.* 1. **電子伏特 /電子伏特, 電子伏 /電子伏** — A unit for measuring the energy of subatomic particles; the energy equal to that attained by an electron moving through a potential difference of one volt. Equivalent to 1.6022 × 10⁻¹⁹ joules.
2. **電子伏特** — a unit of energy equal to the work done by an electron accelerated through a potential difference of 1 volt

<a id="entry-e-000857"></a>
## electronegative

*adj.* 1. **帶負電+的, 帶負電荷+的, 負+的, 陰性+的** — having a negative charge
*“electrons are negative”*

<a id="entry-e-000858"></a>
## electronegativity

*n.* 1. **電負性 /電負性** — The tendency, or a measure of the ability, of an atom or molecule to attract electrons when forming bonds.
2. (chemistry) the tendency of an atom or radical to attract electrons in the formation of an ionic bond

<a id="entry-e-000859"></a>
## electroneutral

*adj.* 1. **電中性+的, 中性+的** — having no net electric charge

<a id="entry-e-000860"></a>
## electronic

**發音：** /ˌɛl.ɛkˈtɹɒn.ɪk/
*adj.* 1. **電子+的** — of or relating to electronics; concerned with or using devices that operate on principles governing the behavior of electrons
*“electronic devices”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **電子的 /電子的** — Operating on the physical behavior of electrons, especially in semiconductors.
3. **電子+的** — of or concerned with electrons
*“electronic energy”*
4. *(not-comparable)* **電子的 /電子的** — Generated or transmitted by an electronic device.
5. *(not-comparable)* **電子的 /電子的, 網絡的 /網絡的** — Of or pertaining to the Internet.

<a id="entry-e-000861"></a>
## electronic balance

*n.* 1. **電子天平** — a balance that generates a current proportional to the displacement of the pan

<a id="entry-e-000862"></a>
## electronic bulletin board

*n.* 1. **電子公告系統** — a computer that is running software that allows users to leave messages and access information of general interest

<a id="entry-e-000863"></a>
## electronic cigarette

**發音：** /ɪˌlɛkˈtɹɑnɪk ˈsɪɡəˌɹɛt/
*n.* 1. **電子煙 /電子煙, 電子香煙 /電子香菸** — A battery-powered device used to vaporize substances, primarily nicotine, for recreative inhalation.

<a id="entry-e-000864"></a>
## electronic computer

*n.* 1. **電子計算機, 電腦, 計算機** — a machine for performing calculations automatically

<a id="entry-e-000865"></a>
## electronic converter

*n.* 1. **電子變換器** — (telecommunication) converter for converting a signal from one frequency to another

<a id="entry-e-000866"></a>
## electronic dance music

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電子舞曲 /電子舞曲** — A field of modern popular music, encompassing house, techno and other genres of percussive dance music whose creation involves electronic devices.

<a id="entry-e-000867"></a>
## electronic data processing

*n.* 1. **電子數據處理** — automatic data processing by electronic means without the use of tabulating cards or punched tapes

<a id="entry-e-000868"></a>
## electronic device

*n.* 1. **電子器件** — a device that accomplishes its purpose electronically
