## electronics

**發音：** /ˌɛl.ɛkˈtɹɒn.ɪks/
*n.* 1. **電子學** — the branch of physics that deals with the emission and effects of electrons and with the use of electronic devices
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **電子學 /電子學** — The study and use of electrical devices that operate by controlling the flow of electrons or other electrically charged particles or by converting the flow of charged particles to or from other forms of energy.
3. *(countable, in-plural, uncountable)* **電子產品 /電子產品** — Devices which require the flow of electrons through conductors and semiconductors in order to perform their function; devices that operate on electrical power (battery or outlet)

<a id="entry-e-000883"></a>
## electrophile

*n.* 1. **親電體 /親電體** — A compound or functional group that is attracted to, and accepts electrons, especially accepting an electron pair from a nucleophile to form a bond.

<a id="entry-e-000884"></a>
## electrophoresis

*n.* 1. **電泳, 電泳分離法, 電解滲入法** — the motion of charged particles in a colloid under the influence of an electric field; particles with a positive charge go to the cathode and negative to the anode
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **電泳 /電泳** — The separation and analysis of large molecules (such as proteins) by migrating a colloidal solution of them through a gel; gel electrophoresis.

<a id="entry-e-000885"></a>
## electrophoretic

*adj.* 1. **電泳+的** — of or relating to electrophoresis

<a id="entry-e-000886"></a>

2. type genus of the family Electrophoridae; electric eels

<a id="entry-e-000887"></a>
## electrophysiology

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電生理學 /電生理學** — The branch of physiology that studies the electrical activity of organisms.

<a id="entry-e-000888"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000891"></a>

2. **驗電器** — measuring instrument that detects electric charge; two gold leaves diverge owing to repulsion of charges with like sign

<a id="entry-e-000892"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000896"></a>

<a id="entry-e-000897"></a>

## element

**發音：** /ˈɛlɪmənt/
*n.* 1. **元件, 組成部分, 零件, 成分, 組件** — an artifact that is one of the individual parts of which a composite entity is made up; especially a part that can be separated from or attached to a system
*“spare components for cars”*
*“a component or constituent element of a system”*
2. **元素, 化學元素** — any of the more than 100 known substances (of which 92 occur naturally) that cannot be separated into simpler substances and that singly or in combination constitute all matter
3. **元素** — A chemical substance made entirely of one such type of atom; any one of the simplest chemical substances that cannot be decomposed in a chemical reaction or by any chemical means and made up of atoms all having the same number of protons.
4. **元素** — One of the four basic building blocks of matter in theories of ancient philosophers and alchemists: water, earth, fire, and air.
5. **四行, 四要素, 元素** — one of four substances thought in ancient and medieval cosmology to constitute the physical universe
*“the alchemists believed that there were four elements”*
6. **元素** — One of the objects in a set.

## elemental

**發音：** /(ˌ)ɛləˈmɛntəl/
*adj.* 1. **元素+的, 未化合+的, 基本+的, 單一+的** — relating to or being an element
*“elemental sulphur”*
2. **粗獷+的, 自然力+的, 原始+的** — relating to severe atmospheric conditions
*“a race against hail or cold rains or some other elemental catastrophe"- J.K.Howard”*
3. of or being the essential or basic part
*“an elementary need for love and nurturing”*
*n.* 1. **元素** — A creature (usually a spirit) that is attuned with, or composed of, one of the classical elements: air, earth, fire and water or variations of them like ice, lightning, etc. They sometimes have unique proper names and sometimes are referred to as Air, Earth, Fire, or Water.

3. easy and not involved or complicated
*“an elementary problem in statistics”*
*“elementary, my dear Watson”*

*“The new law lifted many people from poverty”*

3. increased in amount or degree
*“raised temperature”*

2. **地平緯度, 海拔** — angular distance above the horizon (especially of a celestial object)

## elicit

**發音：** /ɪˈlɪsɪt/
*v.* 1. **使出現, 喚起, 引起** — call forth (emotions, feelings, and responses)
*“arouse pity”*
*“raise a smile”*
2. **引出** — To evoke, educe (emotions, feelings, responses, etc.); to generate, obtain, or provoke as a response or answer.
3. **推斷 /推斷, 推理** — To use logic to arrive at truth; to derive by reason.
4. deduce (a principle) or construe (a meaning)
*“We drew out some interesting linguistic data from the native informant”*
5. derive by reason
*“elicit a solution”*

*“an elicited response”*

*“Let's eliminate the course on Akkadian hieroglyphics”*
*“Socialism extinguished these archaic customs”*
2. **除去, 消除, 廢除, 排除** — do away with
3. **排除, 駁回** — dismiss from consideration or a contest
*“John was ruled out as a possible suspect because he had a strong alibi”*
*“This possibility can be eliminated from our consideration”*
4. *(transitive)* **消滅 /消滅, 消除, 根除** — To completely remove, get rid of, put an end to.
5. *(slang, transitive)* **抹殺 /抹殺, 除掉, 殺 /殺** — To kill (a person or animal).
6. *(ambitransitive)* **排出** — To excrete (waste products).

<a id="entry-e-000961"></a>
## elimination

2. **排洩** — the bodily process of discharging waste matter
3. **消去, 除去** — the act of removing an unknown mathematical quantity by combining equations
4. analysis of a problem into alternative possibilities followed by the systematic rejection of unacceptable alternatives
5. the murder of a competitor

## ellipse

2. **橢圓, 橢圓形** — a closed plane curve resulting from the intersection of a circular cone and a plane cutting completely through it
*“the sums of the distances from the foci to any point on an ellipse is constant”*

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **電子郵件 /電子郵件, 電郵 /電郵** — A system for sending messages and datas by means of a computer network, primarily the Internet, using the Simple Mail Transfer Protocol and the Internet Message Format.

<a id="entry-e-001083"></a>
## emblematic

*adj.* 1. **標誌+的, 象徵性+的, 象徵派+的, 象徵+的, 典型+的** — serving as a visible symbol for something abstract
*“a crown is emblematic of royalty”*

2. **典型+的, 象徵+的** — being or serving as an illustration of a type
*“the free discussion that is emblematic of democracy”*
*“an action exemplary of his conduct”*

<a id="entry-e-001115"></a>
## emerge

**發音：** /ɪˈmɜːd͡ʒ/
*v.* 1. **暴露, 顯現, 浮現, 出現** — come out into view, as from concealment
*“Suddenly, the proprietor emerged from his office”*
2. **暴露, 顯現, 浮現, 出現** — become known or apparent
*“Some nice results emerged from the study”*
3. **顯出, 浮現, 暴露, 出現** — come up to the surface of or rise
*“He felt new emotions emerge”*
4. *(intransitive)* **出現 /出現** — To come into view.
5. *(copulative, intransitive)* **出來 /出來** — To come out of a situation, object, or a liquid.
6. **顯現出來, 浮現, 出現, 形成** — happen or occur as a result of something

<a id="entry-e-001116"></a>
## emergence

**發音：** /ɪˈmɜː.d͡ʒ(ə)ns/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **湧現 /湧現, 出現 /出現, 突現 /突現** — The arising of emergent structure in complex systems.
2. the gradual beginning or coming forth
*“figurines presage the emergence of sculpture in Greece”*
3. the becoming visible
*“not a day's difference between the emergence of the andrenas and the opening of the willow catkins”*
4. the act of emerging
5. the act of coming (or going) out; becoming apparent

<a id="entry-e-001117"></a>
## emergency

**發音：** /ɪˈmɜː(ɹ).dʒən.si/
*n.* 1. **緊急事故, 緊急情況** — a sudden unforeseen crisis (usually involving danger) that requires immediate action
*“he never knew what to do in an emergency”*
2. **非常時刻, 緊急情況** — a state in which martial law applies
*“the governor declared a state of emergency”*
3. *(attributive, sometimes)* **緊急事故 /緊急事故, 緊急情況 /緊急情況, 急診 /急診, 應急 /應急** — A situation which poses an immediate risk and which requires urgent attention.
4. **急診室 /急診室, 急症室** — The department of a hospital that treats emergencies.
5. **手制動器, 手力制動器, 手閘** — a brake operated by hand; usually operates by mechanical linkage

*adj.* 1. **加強語氣+的, 著重+的** — spoken with emphasis
*“an emphatic word”*
2. **強調** — Characterized by emphasis; forceful.
3. sudden and strong
*“an emphatic no”*
4. forceful and definite in expression or action
*“the document contained a particularly emphatic guarantee of religious liberty”*

<a id="entry-e-001193"></a>
## emphysema

**發音：** /ˌɛmfɪˈsiːmə/
*n.* 1. **氣腫, 肺氣腫** — an abnormal condition of the lungs marked by decreased respiratory function; associated with smoking or chronic bronchitis or old age
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **肺氣腫 /肺氣腫** — An abnormal accumulation of air or other gas in tissues, most commonly the lungs.

<a id="entry-e-001194"></a>
## emphysematous gangrene

<a id="entry-e-001195"></a>

*n.* 1. **氣性壞疽** — (pathology) a deadly form of gangrene usually caused by clostridium bacteria that produce toxins that cause tissue death; can be used as a bioweapon

<a id="entry-e-001196"></a>
## empire

**發音：** /ˈɛm.paɪ̯ə̯/
*n.* 1. **帝國 /帝國** — A political unit ruled by an emperor or empress.
2. **帝國 /帝國** — The group of states or other territories that owe allegiance to an imperial power (foreign to them), when distinguished from the native territory of that power; imperial possessions.
3. **帝國** — a monarchy with an emperor as head of state
4. **帝國 /帝國** — An expansive and powerful enterprise under the control of one person or group.
5. **企業集團, 聯合企業, 聯合大企業** — a group of diverse companies under common ownership and run as a single organization
6. the domain ruled by an emperor or empress; the region over which imperial dominion is exercised

<a id="entry-e-001197"></a>
## Empire of Japan

*prop. n.* 1. *(historical)* **大日本帝國 /大日本帝國** — Japan from the Constitution of the Empire of Japan (1890) until the enactment of the post-WWII Constitution of Japan (1947). Unofficially used for Japan from the end of the shogunate (1868) to the end of World War II (1945).

<a id="entry-e-001198"></a>
## Empire State Building

*prop. n.* 1. **帝國大廈 /帝國大廈** — A skyscraper in New York City, the tallest in the world from 1931–72.

## empiric

*adj.* 1. **江湖醫生+的, 經驗主義+的, 經驗主義者** — relying on medical quackery
*“empiric treatment”*
2. **經驗主義+的, 經驗主義者** — derived from experiment and observation rather than theory
*“an empirical basis for an ethical theory”*
*“empirical laws”*

<a id="entry-e-001200"></a>
## empirical

**發音：** /ɪmˈpɪɹɪkəl/
*adj.* 1. **經驗主義+的, 經驗主義者** — derived from experiment and observation rather than theory
*“an empirical basis for an ethical theory”*
*“empirical laws”*
2. **經驗 /經驗** — Pertaining to or based on experience, as opposed to theory.
3. **江湖醫生+的, 經驗主義+的, 經驗主義者** — relying on medical quackery
*“empiric treatment”*
4. **實證 /實證** — Verifiable by means of scientific experimentation.

<a id="entry-e-001201"></a>
## empirical data

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **權威資料 /權威資料** — Data derived from reliable measurement or observation.

<a id="entry-e-001202"></a>
## empirical formula

*n.* 1. **實驗式 /實驗式, 最簡式 /最簡式** — A notation indicating the ratios of the various elements present in a compound, without regard to the actual numbers.
2. a chemical formula showing the ratio of elements in a compound rather than the total number of atoms

<a id="entry-e-001203"></a>
## empirical research

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **實證研究 /實證研究** — Research that bases its findings on direct or indirect observation as its test of reality.
2. an empirical search for knowledge

<a id="entry-e-001204"></a>
## empiricism

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **經驗主義 /經驗主義, 經驗論 /經驗論** — A doctrine which holds that the only or, at least, the most reliable source of human knowledge is experience, especially perception by means of the physical senses. (Often contrasted with rationalism.)
2. (philosophy) the doctrine that knowledge derives from experience
3. the application of empirical methods in any art or science
4. medical practice and advice based on observation and experience in ignorance of scientific findings

*“the end of the year”*

*“the ends justify the means”*
3. **死亡** — a final state
*“he came to a bad end”*

4. **盡頭** — a boundary marking the extremities of something
*“the end of town”*
5. **結束 /結束, 末尾, 端** — The most extreme point of an object, especially one that is longer than it is wide.
6. **總結, 結束, 結論** — the last section of a communication

2. *(transitive)* **危害, 危及, 使受危險 /使受危險** — To put (someone or something) in danger; to risk causing harm to.
3. put in a dangerous, disadvantageous, or difficult position

<a id="entry-e-001336"></a>
## endangered

**發音：** /ɪnˈdeɪn.d͡ʒəd/

*“an endangered species”*
2. **瀕危 /瀕危** — in danger, at risk, said of something where there is a strong possibility something bad will happen to it (for example, a species in danger of going extinct)

<a id="entry-e-001337"></a>
## endangered species

2. a species whose numbers are so small that the species is at risk of extinction

<a id="entry-e-001338"></a>

*“drinking alcohol is a health hazard”*

<a id="entry-e-001339"></a>

*n.* 1. **動脈內膜炎** — inflammation of the inner lining of an artery

<a id="entry-e-001340"></a>

<a id="entry-e-001341"></a>
## endeavor

**發音：** /ɪnˈdɛv.ə/
*n.* 1. **盡力, 努力** — a purposeful or industrious undertaking (especially one that requires effort or boldness)
*“he had doubts about the whole enterprise”*
2. **力圖, 努力, 盡力, 嘗試** — earnest and conscientious activity intended to do or accomplish something
*“made an effort to cover all the reading material”*
*“wished him luck in his endeavor”*
3. *(US)* **努力, 使命, 企業, 事業 /事業** — A sincere attempt; a determined or assiduous effort towards a specific goal; assiduous or persistent activity.
*v.* 1. **企圖, 力爭, 力圖, 力求, 盡力** — attempt by employing effort
*“we endeavor to make our customers happy”*
2. *(US, intransitive)* **努力** — To attempt through application of effort (to do something); to try strenuously.

*“he had doubts about the whole enterprise”*
2. **力圖, 努力, 盡力, 嘗試** — earnest and conscientious activity intended to do or accomplish something

*“we endeavor to make our customers happy”*

<a id="entry-e-001343"></a>
## endemic

**發音：** /ɛnˈdɛm.ɪk/
*n.* 1. **地方病** — a disease that is constantly present to a greater or lesser degree in people of a certain class or in people living in a particular location
2. **地方病, 風土病** — A disease affecting a number of people simultaneously, so as to show a distinct connection with certain localities.
3. **地方特有種** — An individual or species that is endemic to a region.
4. a plant that is native to a certain limited area
*“it is an endemic found only this island”*
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **地方特有的** — Native to a particular area or culture; originating where it occurs.
2. *(especially, not-comparable)* **地方性流行, 地方特有的** — Peculiar to a particular area or region; not found in other places.
3. *(especially, not-comparable)* **地方特有的** — Prevalent in a particular area or region, persistent within a population.
4. **土生+的, 本土+的, 土著+的** — originating where it is found
*“the autochthonal fauna of Australia includes the kangaroo”*
*“autochthonous rocks and people and folktales”*
5. of or relating to a disease (or anything resembling a disease) constantly present to greater or lesser extent in a particular locality
*“diseases endemic to the tropics”*
*“endemic malaria”*
6. native to or confined to a certain region
*“the islands have a number of interesting endemic species”*

<a id="entry-e-001344"></a>
## endemic disease

*n.* 1. **地方病** — a disease that is constantly present to a greater or lesser degree in people of a certain class or in people living in a particular location

3. the final stages of a chess game after most of the pieces have been removed from the board

**發音：** /ˈɛndɪŋ/
*n.* 1. **結束 /結束** — A termination or conclusion.
2. **結局 /結局** — The last part of something.
3. **末尾, 末期, 端, 終止, 結束** — the point in time at which something ends
*“the end of the year”*
*“the ending of warranty period”*
4. **收場, 最後階段, 終止, 結局, 結束** — event whose occurrence ends something
*“his death marked the ending of an era”*
*“when these final episodes are broadcast it will be the finish of the show”*
5. **總結, 結束, 結論** — the last section of a communication
*“in conclusion I want to say...”*
6. the end of a word (a suffix or inflectional ending or final morpheme)
*“I don't like words that have -ism as an ending”*

## endive

**發音：** /ˈɛndaɪv/

2. widely cultivated herb with leaves valued as salad green; either curly serrated leaves or broad flat ones that are usually blanched
3. variety of endive having leaves with irregular frilled edges

<a id="entry-e-001352"></a>
## endless

**發音：** /ˈɛndlɪs/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **無窮 /無窮, 不盡 /不盡** — Having no end.
2. tiresomely long; seemingly without end
*“endless debates”*
*“an endless conversation”*
3. infinitely great in number
*“endless waves”*
4. having no known beginning and presumably no end
*“the dateless rise and fall of the tides”*
*“time is endless”*
5. having the ends united so as to form a continuous whole
*“an endless chain”*

<a id="entry-e-001353"></a>
## endlessly

**發音：** /ˈɛndləsli/
*adv.* 1. **不休, 不斷地 /不斷地, 不已** — In an endless manner; continuously without limit.
2. **無垠地, 無界限地** — (spatial sense) without bounds
*“the Nubian desert seemed to stretch out before them endlessly”*
3. continuing forever without end
*“there are infinitely many possibilities”*
4. with unflagging resolve
*“dance inspires him ceaselessly to strive higher and higher toward the shining pinnacle of perfection that is the goal of every artiste”*
5. all the time; seemingly without stopping
*“a theological student with whom I argued interminably”*
*“her nagging went on endlessly”*

*“We went without water and food for 3 days”*
*“These superstitions survive in the backwaters of America”*
4. **忍耐, 蒙受, 遭受, 遭到, 忍受** — undergo or be subjected to
*“He suffered the penalty”*
*“Many saints suffered martyrdom”*
5. *(intransitive)* **承受, 忍受** — To continue or carry on, despite obstacles or hardships; to persist.
6. *(transitive)* **忍受** — To tolerate or put up with something unpleasant.

<a id="entry-e-001423"></a>
## energy

**發音：** /ˈɛn.ə.d͡ʒi/
*n.* 1. **精力, 活力** — forceful exertion
*“he plays tennis with great energy”*
*“he's full of zip”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **能量, 能源, 精力** — The impetus behind all motion and all activity.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **能量** — The capacity to do work.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **能, 能量** — A quantity that denotes the ability to do work and is measured in a unit dimensioned in mass × distance²/time² (ML²/T²) or the equivalent.
5. **持續力, 活力, 生命力, 生存能力, 蓬勃的生氣** — a healthy capacity for vigorous activity
*“jogging works off my excess energy”*
*“he seemed full of vim and vigor”*
6. (physics) a thermodynamic quantity equivalent to the capacity of a physical system to do work; the units of energy are joules or ergs
*“energy can take a wide variety of forms”*

*“according to quantum theory only certain energy levels are possible”*

*prop. n.* 1. **啟蒙時代 /啟蒙時代** — A 17th- and 18th-century European intellectual and cultural movement emphasizing rationalism. The period during which it flourished is called the Age of Enlightenment or Age of Reason.

2. **入寂, 涅槃, 涅盤** — (Hinduism and Buddhism) the beatitude that transcends the cycle of reincarnation; characterized by the extinction of desire and suffering and individual consciousness

*“the Enlightenment brought about many humanitarian reforms”*

*“This matter entrenches on other domains”*
2. *(ambitransitive, literally)* **挖掘壕溝** — To surround or provide with a trench, especially for defense; to dig in.
3. *(ambitransitive, figuratively)* **鞏固** — To establish a substantial position in business, politics, etc.
4. fix firmly or securely
5. occupy a trench or secured area
*“The troops dug in for the night”*

<a id="entry-e-001663"></a>
## entropy

**發音：** /ˈɛntɹəpi/
*n.* 1. *(countable)* **熵** — A measure of the amount of energy in a physical system that cannot be used to do work.
2. *(countable)* **熵** — A measure of the amount of information and noise present in a signal.
3. *(uncountable)* **熵** — The tendency of a system that is left to itself to descend into chaos.
4. (communication theory) a numerical measure of the uncertainty of an outcome
*“the signal contained thousands of bits of information”*
5. (thermodynamics) a thermodynamic quantity representing the amount of energy in a system that is no longer available for doing mechanical work
*“entropy increases as matter and energy in the universe degrade to an ultimate state of inert uniformity”*

<a id="entry-e-001664"></a>

3. **交託, 信託, 交付** — put into the care or protection of someone

*“leave your child the nurse's care”*

<a id="entry-e-001689"></a>

## epic

2. a long narrative poem telling of a hero's deeds

2. **本輪** — a circle that rolls around (inside or outside) another circle; generates an epicycloid or hypocycloid

## epidemic

**發音：** /ˌɛpɪˈdɛmɪk/

2. a widespread outbreak of an infectious disease; many people are infected at the same time
*adj.* 1. (especially of medicine) of disease or anything resembling a disease; attacking or affecting many individuals in a community or a population simultaneously
*“an epidemic outbreak of influenza”*

<a id="entry-e-001763"></a>

<a id="entry-e-001764"></a>

<a id="entry-e-001765"></a>

<a id="entry-e-001766"></a>

<a id="entry-e-001767"></a>

<a id="entry-e-001768"></a>

<a id="entry-e-001769"></a>

<a id="entry-e-001770"></a>

## epilepsia major

2. **大發作** — a seizure during which the patient becomes unconscious and has convulsions over the entire body

<a id="entry-e-001802"></a>
## epilepsia minor

*“the girl was frightened by her first petit mal”*
2. epilepsy characterized by paroxysmal attacks of brief clouding of consciousness (and possibly other abnormalities)
*“she has been suffering from petit mal since childhood”*

## epilepsy

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **羊癲瘋 /羊癲瘋, 癲癇 /癲癇, 癲癇癥 /癲癇症, 羊癇 /羊癇, 羊癇瘋 /羊癇瘋, 羊角風 /羊角風** — A medical condition in which the sufferer experiences seizures (or convulsions) and blackouts.
2. **癲癇, 癲癇症** — a disorder of the central nervous system characterized by loss of consciousness and convulsions

## episode

**發音：** /ˈɛp.ɪ.səʊd/
*n.* 1. **事件, 插曲** — A discrete but connected event, situation, or period within a broader sequence, narrative, or life course.
2. **集** — A bounded narrative unit within a television series, constituting a single installment that is structurally discrete yet interrelated with the series as a whole.
3. **一集** — a part of a broadcast serial
4. a happening that is distinctive in a series of related events
5. a brief section of a literary or dramatic work that forms part of a connected series
6. film consisting of a succession of related shots that develop a given subject in a movie

<a id="entry-e-001830"></a>
## episodic

*“an account concerned primarily with episodic events such as the succession of rulers”*

## epoch

**發音：** /ˈiːpɒk/
*n.* 1. **年代, 時期, 代, 時代, 紀元** — a period marked by distinctive character or reckoned from a fixed point or event
2. **時代 /時代, 會 /會** — A particular period of history, or of a person's life, especially one considered noteworthy or remarkable.
3. **曆元** — (astronomy) an arbitrarily fixed date that is the point in time relative to which information (as coordinates of a celestial body) is recorded
4. **紀元, 時期** — a unit of geological time that is a subdivision of a period and is itself divided into ages
5. **世** — A geochronologic unit of hundreds of thousands to millions of years; a subdivision of a period, and subdivided into ages (or sometimes subepochs).

*“epochal decisions made by Roosevelt and Churchill”*

## equal

**發音：** /ˈiːkwəl/
*adj.* 1. **充分+的, 足夠+的, 適當+的** — having the requisite qualities or resources to meet a task
*“she had adequate training”*
*“her training was adequate”*
2. *(not-comparable, usually)* **平等, 同等** — The same in all respects that matter practically; interchangeable, fungible, or (even sometimes) identical for practical purposes.
3. *(not-comparable, usually)* **等於 /等於, 等量** — Exactly identical, having the same value.
4. having the same quantity, value, or measure as another
*“on equal terms”*
*“all men are equal before the law”*
*v.* 1. **等於, 等同, 是** — be identical or equivalent to
*“One dollar equals 1,000 rubles these days!”*
2. **比得上** — be equal to in quality or ability
*“Nothing can rival cotton for durability”*
*“Your performance doesn't even touch that of your colleagues”*
3. *(copulative)* **等於 /等於, 倫比 /倫比, 匹敵 /匹敵** — To be equal to, to have the same value as; to correspond to.
4. **使均等, 使相等** — make equal, uniform, corresponding, or matching
*“let's equalize the duties among all employees in this office”*
*“The company matched the discount policy of its competitors”*
*n.* 1. **同行, 同輩, 地位相等的人, 同齡人** — a person who is of equal standing with another in a group

## equilibrium

**發音：** /ɛkwɪˈlɪbɹɪəm/
*n.* 1. **均衡, 平衡** — a stable situation in which forces cancel one another
2. **化學平衡** — a chemical reaction and its reverse proceed at equal rates
3. **平衡** — The condition of a system in which competing influences are balanced, resulting in no net change.
4. **平衡** — The state of a reaction in which the rates of the forward and reverse reactions are the same.
5. **平衡, 均衡** — equality of distribution
6. **平衡** — The state of a body at rest or in uniform motion in which the resultant of all forces on it is zero.

## equinox

**發音：** /ˈɛkwɪnɒks/
*n.* 1. **二分點 /二分點, 分點 /分點, 晝夜平分點 /晝夜平分點** — One of two times in the year (one in March and the other in September) when the length of the day and the night are equal, which occurs when the sun is directly overhead at the equator; this marks the beginning of spring in one hemisphere and autumn in the other.
2. **春分或秋分, 晝夜平分點** — either of two times of the year when the sun crosses the plane of the earth's equator and day and night are of equal length
3. (astronomy) either of the two celestial points at which the celestial equator intersects the ecliptic

## equity

**發音：** /ˈɛk.wɪ.ti/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **公平, 公平性, 公正, 公正性** — Fairness, impartiality, or justice as determined in light of "natural law" or "natural right".
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **衡平, 衡平法** — The power of a court of law having extra-statutory discretion, to decide legal matters and to provide legal relief apart from, though not in violation of, the prevailing legal code; in some cases, a court "sitting in equity" may provide relief to a complainant should the code be found either inapplicable or insufficient to do so.
3. **股票值, 股份** — the ownership interest of shareholders in a corporation
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **淨值 /淨值** — Value of property minus liens or other encumbrances.
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **股權 /股權, 權益 /權益** — Ownership, especially in terms of net monetary value of some business.
6. *(countable, uncountable)* **股權 /股權, 權益 /權益** — Ownership interest in a company as determined by subtracting liabilities from assets.

<a id="entry-e-001934"></a>
## equivalence

**發音：** /ɪˈkwɪv.ə.ləns/
*n.* 1. **平等, 相等, 相等的狀態, 同等** — a state of being essentially equal or equivalent; equally balanced
*“on a par with the best”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **對等 /對等, 對應 /對應** — The degree to which a term or text in one language is semantically similar to its translated counterpart.
3. essential equality and interchangeability
4. qualities that are comparable
*“no comparison between the two books”*
*“beyond compare”*

<a id="entry-e-001935"></a>
## equivalent

**發音：** /ɪˈkwɪvələnt/
*n.* 1. **當量 /當量** — Anything that is virtually equal to something else, or has the same value, force, etc.
2. a person or thing equal to another in value or measure or force or effect or significance etc
*“send two dollars or the equivalent in stamps”*
3. the atomic weight of an element that has the same combining capacity as a given weight of another element; the standard is 8 for oxygen
*adj.* 1. **等值** — Similar or identical in value, meaning or effect; virtually equal.
2. **等價的** — Of two sets, having a one-to-one correspondence.
3. being essentially equal to something
*“it was as good as gold”*
*“a wish that was equivalent to a command”*

<a id="entry-e-001936"></a>
## equivalent-binary-digit factor

*n.* 1. **二進制平均數** — the average number of binary digits needed to express one radix digit in a numeration system that is not binary; on the average a number that can be expressed in N decimal digits takes 3.3N binary digits

<a id="entry-e-001937"></a>
## equivocal

*adj.* 1. **意義不明確+的, 模稜兩可+的** — open to two or more interpretations; or of uncertain nature or significance; or (often) intended to mislead
*“an equivocal statement”*
*“the polling had a complex and equivocal (or ambiguous) message for potential female candidates”*
2. open to question
*“aliens of equivocal loyalty”*
*“his conscience reproached him with the equivocal character of the union into which he had forced his son"-Anna Jameson”*
3. uncertain as a sign or indication
*“the evidence from bacteriologic analysis was equivocal”*

<a id="entry-e-001938"></a>
## equivocalness

*n.* 1. **含糊不清, 模稜兩可** — unclearness by virtue of having more than one meaning

<a id="entry-e-001939"></a>
## equivocation

**發音：** /ɪˌkwɪvəˈkeɪʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **偷換概念 /偷換概念** — A logical fallacy resulting from the use of multiple meanings of a single expression.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **模稜兩可 /模稜兩可** — The use of expressions susceptible of a double signification, possibly intentionally and with the aim of misleading.
3. a statement that is not literally false but that cleverly avoids an unpleasant truth
4. intentionally vague or ambiguous
5. falsification by means of vague or ambiguous language

## eradicate

**發音：** /ɪˈɹæd.ɪ.keɪt/
*v.* 1. *(archaic, transitive)* **連根拔起 /連根拔起** — To pull up by the roots.
2. *(transitive)* **根除** — To destroy completely; to reduce to nothing radically; to put an end to.
3. kill in large numbers
*“the plague wiped out an entire population”*
4. destroy completely, as if down to the roots
*“the vestiges of political democracy were soon uprooted" "root out corruption”*

*“a signal too loud to be erasable in a single pass through the erase head”*

<a id="entry-e-001957"></a>

**發音：** /ɪˈɹeɪs/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **拭除, 抹去, 擦去** — To remove (markings or information).
2. **刪除, 抹去, 拭除, 擦去, 擦除** — wipe out digitally or magnetically recorded information
*“Who erased the files form my hard disk?”*
3. remove from memory or existence
*“The Turks erased the Armenians in 1915”*
4. remove by or as if by rubbing or erasing
*“Please erase the formula on the blackboard--it is wrong!”*

## ergosterol

## ergot

<a id="entry-e-001996"></a>
## ergotamine

**發音：** /ɜːˈɡɒtəmiːn/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **麥角胺 /麥角胺** — An alkaloid, extracted from ergot, that causes constriction of blood vessels and has been used to treat migraine; it is related to lysergic acid.
2. **麥角胺** — an alkaloid derived from ergot that is less toxic than ergot; causes constriction of blood vessels and is used to treat migraine

<a id="entry-e-001997"></a>
## ergotic

*adj.* 1. **麥角病+的, 麥角症+的** — relating to or produced by ergot

<a id="entry-e-001998"></a>

*n.* 1. **紅黴素** — an antibiotic (trade name Erythrocin or E-Mycin or Ethril or Ilosone or Pediamycin) obtained from the actinomycete Streptomyces erythreus; effective against many Gram-positive bacteria and some Gram-negative

<a id="entry-e-002125"></a>
## escalate

**發音：** /ˈɛs.kə.leɪt/
*v.* 1. *(ambitransitive)* **升級 /升級, 升高** — To increase (something) in extent or intensity; to intensify or step up.
2. **強化, 逐步升級, 加強** — increase in extent or intensity
*“The Allies escalated the bombing”*

<a id="entry-e-002126"></a>
## escalation

**發音：** /ˌɛs.kəˈleɪ.ʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **升級 /升級, 擴大 /擴大, 擴戰 /擴戰** — A deliberate or premeditated increase in the violence or geographic scope of a conflict.
2. an increase to counteract a perceived discrepancy
*“higher wages caused an escalation of prices”*
*“there was a gradual escalation of hostilities”*

<a id="entry-e-002127"></a>
## escalator

**發音：** /ˈɛs.kə.leɪ.tə/
*n.* 1. **自動扶梯 /自動扶梯, 電動扶梯 /電動扶梯, 自動電梯 /自動電梯, 自動樓梯 /自動樓梯, 自動鏈梯 /自動鏈梯, 升降階梯 /升降階梯** — A motor-driven mechanical device consisting of a continuous loop of steps that automatically conveys people from one floor to another.
2. **升降階梯, 自動扶梯, 自動樓梯, 自動電梯, 自動鏈梯** — a stairway whose steps move continuously on a circulating belt
3. a clause in a contract that provides for an increase or a decrease in wages or prices or benefits etc. depending on certain conditions (as a change in the cost of living index)

## escallop

2. edible muscle of mollusks having fan-shaped shells; served broiled or poached or in salads or cream sauces
3. thin slice of meat (especially veal) usually fried or broiled
*v.* 1. bake in a sauce, milk, etc., often with breadcrumbs on top

<a id="entry-e-002129"></a>
## escapade

*n.* 1. **冒險越軌行為, 危險事業, 大膽的冒險** — a wild and exciting undertaking (not necessarily lawful)
2. **歡樂** — any carefree episode

<a id="entry-e-002130"></a>
## escape

**發音：** /ɪˈskeɪp/
*v.* 1. **逃生, 逃跑, 逃脫** — run away from confinement
*“The convicted murderer escaped from a high security prison”*
2. **使困惑, 使迷惑** — be incomprehensible to; escape understanding by
*“What you are seeing in him eludes me”*
3. *(intransitive)* **逃生, 逃跑** — To get free; to free oneself.
4. *(transitive)* **逃逸** — To avoid (any unpleasant person or thing); to elude, get away from.
5. *(intransitive)* **逃逸** — To avoid capture; to get away with something, avoid punishment.
6. **溜走, 逃脫, 擺脫, 離開** — remove oneself from a familiar environment, usually for pleasure or diversion
*“We escaped to our summer house for a few days”*
*“The president of the company never manages to get away during the summer”*
*n.* 1. **逃脫** — the act of escaping physically
*“he made his escape from the mental hospital”*
*“the canary escaped from its cage”*
2. **空想主義, 逃避主義, 逃避現實** — an inclination to retreat from unpleasant realities through diversion or fantasy
*“romantic novels were her escape from the stress of daily life”*
*“his alcohol problem was a form of escapism”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **逃跑, 逃脫 /逃脫** — The act of leaving a dangerous or unpleasant situation.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **退出鍵 /退出鍵, 逸出鍵 /逸出鍵** — escape key
5. nonperformance of something distasteful (as by deceit or trickery) that you are supposed to do
*“his evasion of his clear duty was reprehensible”*
*“that escape from the consequences is possible but unattractive”*
6. an avoidance of danger or difficulty
*“that was a narrow escape”*

<a id="entry-e-002131"></a>
## escape character

*n.* 1. **轉義字符** — A single metacharacter, usually a control code, which in a sequence of characters signifies that what is to follow takes an alternative interpretation. The term escape sequence refers to the escape character and the subsequent character or characters so modified.

<a id="entry-e-002132"></a>
## escape from

*v.* 1. **擺脫, 甩掉, 逃避** — get rid of
*“I couldn't shake the car that was following me”*

<a id="entry-e-002133"></a>
## escape hatch

*n.* 1. **安全艙口** — hatchway that provides a means of escape in an emergency

<a id="entry-e-002134"></a>

<a id="entry-e-002135"></a>

<a id="entry-e-002136"></a>

<a id="entry-e-002137"></a>
## escape sequence

*n.* 1. **轉義序列 /轉義序列** — An escape character together with subsequent characters, not output as written, but specifying a particular behaviour.

## estradiol

**發音：** /ˌɛs.tɹəˈdaɪˌɔl/
*n.* 1. *(US, countable, uncountable)* **雌二醇** — A natural estrogenic hormone that is a phenolic alcohol estra-1,3,5-triene-3,17-diol (C₁₈H₂₄O₂) secreted chiefly by the ovaries of all vertebrates, that is a major and the most potent of the naturally occurring estrogens; the semisynthetic esterified compound is used medicinally to treat estrogen deficiency and breast cancer.
2. **雌二醇, 雌甾二醇** — the most powerful female hormone that occurs naturally; synthesized and used to treat estrogen deficiency and breast cancer

<a id="entry-e-002251"></a>
## estradiol patch

*n.* 1. **雌二醇貼片** — a transdermal patch that allows estradiol to be absorbed into the blood stream; used in treating estrogen deficiency and in hormone replacement therapy

<a id="entry-e-002252"></a>
## estradiol valerate

**發音：** [ˈɛ.stɹəˌda͡ɪ.oʟ ˈvæ.lɚ.ɪt]
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **戊酸雌二醇** — The estradiol ester of valeric acid that is an estradiol medication - C₂₃H₃₂O₃.

<a id="entry-e-002253"></a>
## estrange

**發音：** /ɪˈstɹeɪnd͡ʒ/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **疏遠 /疏遠** — To cause to feel less close or friendly; alienate. To cease contact with (particularly of a family member or spouse, especially in form estranged).
2. **使疏遠, 使離開, 離間** — remove from customary environment or associations
*“years of boarding school estranged the child from her home”*
3. arouse hostility or indifference in where there had formerly been love, affection, or friendliness
*“She alienated her friends when she became fanatically religious”*

<a id="entry-e-002254"></a>
## estranged

*v.* 1. *(form-of, participle, past)* **疏遠 /疏遠** — simple past and past participle of estrange

<a id="entry-e-002255"></a>

2. **疏遠, 離間, 不和** — the feeling of being alienated from other people

## ether

**發音：** /ˈiː.θə/
*n.* 1. **醚** — any of a class of organic compounds that have two hydrocarbon groups linked by an oxygen atom
2. **乙醚** — a colorless volatile highly inflammable liquid formerly used as an inhalation anesthetic
3. *(uncountable)* **乙醚** — Diethyl ether (C₄H₁₀O), an organic compound with a sweet odour used in the past as an anaesthetic.
4. *(countable)* **醚** — Any of a class of organic compounds containing an oxygen atom bonded to two hydrocarbon groups.
5. **第五種要素** — the fifth and highest element after air and earth and fire and water; was believed to be the substance composing all heavenly bodies
6. a medium that was once supposed to fill all space and to support the propagation of electromagnetic waves

<a id="entry-e-002308"></a>
## ethereal

*“figures light and aeriform come unlooked for and melt away"- Thomas Carlyle”*
*“aerial fancies”*
2. **空靈 /空靈** — Pertaining to the presupposition of an invisible air-like element permeating all of space, or to the higher regions beyond the earth or beyond the atmosphere.
3. **乙醚+的, 含乙醚+的** — of or containing or dissolved in ether
*“ethereal solution”*
4. of heaven or the spirit
*“celestial peace”*
*“ethereal melodies”*
5. characterized by unusual lightness and delicacy
*“this smallest and most ethereal of birds”*
*“gossamer shading through his playing”*

## eutrophic

<a id="entry-e-002603"></a>
## eutrophication

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **富營養化 /富營養化** — The process of becoming eutrophic; the ecosystem's response to the addition of artificial or natural nutrients, mainly phosphates, through detergents, fertilizers, or sewage, to an aquatic system.
2. excessive nutrients in a lake or other body of water, usually caused by runoff of nutrients (animal waste, fertilizers, sewage) from the land, which causes a dense growth of plant life; the decomposition of the plants depletes the supply of oxygen, leading to the death of animal life
*“he argued that the controlling factor in eutrophication is not nitrate but phosphate”*

## evacuate

**發音：** /ɪˈvæk.ju.eɪt/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **撤離 /撤離, 疏散, 後送 /後送** — To leave or withdraw from; to quit; to retire from.
2. **搬出, 撤出, 疏散, 轉移** — move people from their homes or country
3. move out of an unsafe location into safety
*“After the earthquake, residents were evacuated”*
4. empty completely
*“evacuate the bottle”*
5. create a vacuum in (a bulb, flask, reaction vessel)
6. excrete or discharge from the body

<a id="entry-e-002607"></a>
## evacuation

2. *(countable, uncountable)* **搬空** — The act of emptying, clearing of the contents, or discharging, including creating a vacuum.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **撤離 /撤離, 疏散, 後送 /後送** — The act of evacuating; leaving a place in an orderly fashion, especially for safety.
4. **排洩** — the bodily process of discharging waste matter
5. the act of evacuating; leaving a place in an orderly fashion; especially for protection

<a id="entry-e-002608"></a>
## evade

**發音：** /ɪˈveɪd/
*v.* 1. **迴避, 規避, 逃脫, 閃避, 逃避** — escape, either physically or mentally
*“The thief eluded the police”*
*“This difficult idea seems to evade her”*
2. *(transitive)* **閃 /閃, 逃脫 /逃脫, 躲開 /躲開** — To get away from by cunning; to avoid by using dexterity, subterfuge, address, or ingenuity; to cleverly escape from.
3. *(transitive)* **溜走, 脫身 /脫身** — To escape; to slip away; — sometimes with from.
4. **規避, 躲避** — practice evasion
*“This man always hesitates and evades”*
5. avoid or try to avoid fulfilling, answering, or performing (duties, questions, or issues)
*“He dodged the issue”*
*“she skirted the problem”*
6. use cunning or deceit to escape or avoid
*“The con man always evades”*

*“I will have the family jewels appraised by a professional”*
*“access all the factors when taking a risk”*

<a id="entry-e-002633"></a>
## eve

**發音：** /iːv/
*n.* 1. **前夕, 前夜** — The day or night before, usually used for holidays, such as Christmas Eve.
2. **前夕, 前夜, 重大事件關頭** — the period immediately before something
*“on the eve of the French Revolution”*
3. **薄暮, 傍晚, 晚上, 黃昏** — the latter part of the day (the period of decreasing daylight from late afternoon until nightfall)
*“he enjoyed the evening light across the lake”*
4. (Old Testament) Adam's wife in Judeo-Christian mythology: the first woman and mother of the human race; God created Eve from Adam's rib and placed Adam and Eve in the Garden of Eden
5. the day before

## even

**發音：** /ˈiːvən/
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **連 /連, 甚至** — In reality (used to imply an extreme example in the case mentioned).
2. *(not-comparable)* **更, 更加** — Used to emphasise a comparative.
3. used as an intensive especially to indicate something unexpected
*“even an idiot knows that”*
*“declined even to consider the idea”*
4. in spite of; notwithstanding
*“even when he is sick, he works”*
*“even with his head start she caught up with him”*
5. to a greater degree or extent; used with comparisons
*“looked sick and felt even worse”*
*“an even (or still) more interesting problem”*
6. to the full extent
*“loyal even unto death”*
*adj.* 1. **偶數+的, 雙數+的** — divisible by two
2. **平坦+的, 平滑+的** — being level or straight or regular and without variation as e.g. in shape or texture; or being in the same plane or at the same height as something else (i.e. even with)
*“an even application of varnish”*
*“an even floor”*
3. **平坦** — Flat and level.
4. *(not-comparable)* **雙數 /雙數, 偶數 /偶數** — Of an integer, divisible by two.
5. **平均+的** — symmetrically arranged
*“even features”*
*“regular features”*
6. **定期+的** — occurring at fixed intervals
*“a regular beat”*
*“the even rhythm of his breathing”*
*n.* 1. **薄暮, 傍晚, 晚上, 黃昏** — the latter part of the day (the period of decreasing daylight from late afternoon until nightfall)
*“he enjoyed the evening light across the lake”*
*v.* 1. **壓平, 使平坦** — become even or more even
*“even out the surface”*
2. **壓平, 使平坦** — make even or more even
3. make level or straight
*“level the ground”*

## event

**發音：** /ɪˈvɛnt/
*n.* 1. **情況, 狀況** — a special set of circumstances
*“in that event, the first possibility is excluded”*
*“it may rain in which case the picnic will be canceled”*
2. **事件, 活動 /活動** — An occurrence; something that happens.
3. **後果, 效果, 結果, 影響** — a phenomenon that follows and is caused by some previous phenomenon
*“the magnetic effect was greater when the rod was lengthwise”*
*“his decision had depressing consequences for business”*
4. **事件** — A point in spacetime having three spatial coordinates and one temporal coordinate.
5. **事件** — A possible action that the user can perform that is monitored by an application or the operating system (event listener). When an event occurs an event handler is called which performs a specific task.
6. **事件** — A set of some of the possible outcomes; a subset of the sample space.

<a id="entry-e-002666"></a>
## event CG

*n.* 1. *(abbreviation, alt-of, initialism)* **事件CG, 特殊事件CG** — Initialism of event computer graphics; a special, full-screen, high-detail illustration that appears during pivotal story moments, replacing the standard layered backgrounds and character sprites, to emphasize the scene's importance, often unlocked for replay.

*“The landlord wanted to evict the tenants so he banged on the pipes every morning at 3 a.m.”*

<a id="entry-e-002706"></a>
## evidence

**發音：** /ˈɛv.ɪ.dəns/
*n.* 1. **基本原則, 根據** — your basis for belief or disbelief; knowledge on which to base belief
*“the evidence that smoking causes lung cancer is very compelling”*
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **證據 /證據** — Facts or observations presented in support of an assertion.
3. *(uncountable, usually)* **證據 /證據** — Anything admitted by a court to prove or disprove alleged matters of fact in a trial.
4. an indication that makes something evident
*“his trembling was evidence of his fear”*
5. (law) all the means by which any alleged matter of fact whose truth is investigated at judicial trial is established or disproved
*v.* 1. **表示, 論證, 作證, 顯示, 證明** — provide evidence for; stand as proof of; show by one's behavior, attitude, or external attributes
*“His high fever attested to his illness”*
*“The buildings in Rome manifest a high level of architectural sophistication”*
2. **作證, 證明** — provide evidence for
*“The blood test showed that he was the father”*
*“Her behavior testified to her incompetence”*
3. give evidence
*“he was telling on all his former colleague”*

<a id="entry-e-002707"></a>
## evidence-based medicine

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **循證醫學 /循證醫學** — Health care whose policies and practices are derived from the systematic, scientific study of the effectiveness of various treatments.

<a id="entry-e-002708"></a>
## evident

*adj.* 1. **顯而易見+的, 明顯+的** — clearly revealed to the mind or the senses or judgment
*“the effects of the drought are apparent to anyone who sees the parched fields”*
*“evident hostility”*
2. capable of being seen or noticed
*“a discernible change in attitude”*
*“a clearly evident erasure in the manuscript”*

<a id="entry-e-002709"></a>
## evidential

*adj.* 1. **作證據用+的, 證據+的, 證明+的** — serving as or based on evidence
*“evidential signs of a forced entry”*
*“its evidentiary value”*

<a id="entry-e-002710"></a>
## evidentiality

**發音：** /ˌɛ.vɪˌdɛn.ʃiˈælɪti/

<a id="entry-e-002711"></a>
## evidentiary

*adj.* 1. **根據證據+的, 證據+的** — pertaining to or constituting evidence
*“evidentiary technique”*
*“an evidentiary fact”*
2. **作證據用+的, 證據+的, 證明+的** — serving as or based on evidence
*“evidential signs of a forced entry”*
*“its evidentiary value”*

**發音：** /ˈiːvəl/

2. morally objectionable behavior
3. that which causes harm or destruction or misfortune
*“the evil that men do lives after them; the good is oft interred with their bones"- Shakespeare”*
4. the quality of being morally wrong in principle or practice
*“attempts to explain the origin of evil in the world”*
*adj.* 1. **墮落+的, 有惡習+的, 罪惡+的, 邪惡+的** — having the nature of vice
2. **邪惡的 /邪惡的, 壞的 /壞的, 兇狠** — Intending to harm; malevolent.
3. morally bad or wrong
*“evil purposes”*
*“an evil influence”*
4. having or exerting a malignant influence
*“malevolent stars”*
*“a malefic force”*

2. a look that is believed to have the power of inflicting harm

<a id="entry-e-002717"></a>

*“She showed her disappointment”*

*n.* 1. **失去力量, 抽取精華, 挫傷元氣** — altering something (as a legislative act or a statement) in such a manner as to reduce its value
*“the adoption of their amendments would have amounted to an evisceration of the act”*
2. surgical removal of an organ (or the contents of an organ) from a patient
3. the act of removing the bowels or viscera; the act of cutting so as to cause the viscera to protrude

<a id="entry-e-002719"></a>

## evocative

*adj.* 1. **引起聯想+的** — serving to bring to mind
*“cannot forbear to close on this redolent literary note"- Wilder Hobson”*
*“a campaign redolent of machine politics”*

<a id="entry-e-002721"></a>
## evoke

**發音：** /ɪˈvəʊk/
*v.* 1. **使出現, 喚起, 引起** — call forth (emotions, feelings, and responses)
*“arouse pity”*
*“raise a smile”*
2. *(transitive)* **喚起 /喚起** — To cause the manifestation of something (emotion, picture, etc.) in someone's mind or imagination.
3. **使人產生, 使人想起, 喚起** — call to mind
*“this remark evoked sadness”*
4. evoke or provoke to appear or occur
*“Her behavior provoked a quarrel between the couple”*
5. deduce (a principle) or construe (a meaning)
*“We drew out some interesting linguistic data from the native informant”*
6. summon into action or bring into existence, often as if by magic
*“raise the specter of unemployment”*
*“he conjured wild birds in the air”*

<a id="entry-e-002722"></a>
## evoked

*adj.* 1. **喚起+的, 引起+的, 激發+的** — called forth from a latent or potential state by stimulation
*“evoked potentials”*
*“an elicited response”*

<a id="entry-e-002723"></a>
## evolution

**發音：** /ˌiːvəˈluːʃ(ə)n/
*n.* 1. **發展, 成長, 進化, 演變, 生長** — a process in which something passes by degrees to a different stage (especially a more advanced or mature stage)
*“the development of his ideas took many years”*
*“the evolution of Greek civilization”*
2. **發展史, 演變, 進化** — (biology) the sequence of events involved in the evolutionary development of a species or taxonomic group of organisms
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **進化 /進化, 演化** — The transformation of animals, plants and other living things into different forms (now understood as a change in genetic composition) by the accumulation of changes over successive generations.

<a id="entry-e-002724"></a>
## evolutionary

*adj.* 1. **演化+的, 進化+的** — of or relating to or produced by evolution
*“evolutionary biology”*

<a id="entry-e-002725"></a>

## exact

**發音：** /ɪɡˈzækt/
*adj.* 1. **準確+的, 確切+的** — marked by strict and particular and complete accordance with fact
*“an exact mind”*
*“an exact copy”*
2. **確切+的, 精密+的, 準確+的, 精確+的** — (of ideas, images, representations, expressions) characterized by perfect conformity to fact or truth; strictly correct
*“a precise image”*
*“a precise measurement”*
3. **正確 /正確, 準確 /準確, 精密** — Precisely agreeing with a standard, a fact, or the truth; perfectly conforming; neither exceeding nor falling short in any respect.
*v.* 1. claim as due or just
*“The bank demanded payment of the loan”*
2. take as an undesirable consequence of some event or state of affairs
*“the accident claimed three lives”*
*“The hard work took its toll on her”*

<a id="entry-e-002753"></a>
## exactly

**發音：** /ɪɡˈzæk(t).li/
*adv.* 1. **準確+地, 確切+地, 恰好+地, 精確+地** — just as it should be
*“`Precisely, my lord,' he said”*
2. **恰恰** — Without approximation; precisely.
3. **精確+地, 準確+地, 確切+地** — in a precise manner
*“she always expressed herself precisely”*
4. indicating exactness or preciseness
*“he was doing precisely (or exactly) what she had told him to do”*
*“it was just as he said--the jewel was gone”*

*“a man of great exactitude”*

*“tended to romanticize and exaggerate this `gracious Old South' imagery”*

*“He overdid it last night when he did 100 pushups”*

*“the dance involved a deliberate exaggeration of his awkwardness”*

2. *(transitive)* **抬高, 提昇 /提升** — To raise in rank, status etc., to elevate.
3. praise, glorify, or honor

*“The children were thrilled at the prospect of going to the movies”*

<a id="entry-e-002762"></a>
## examination

**發音：** /ɪɡˌzæm.ɪˈneɪ.ʃən/
*n.* 1. **測驗, 考試** — a set of questions or exercises evaluating skill or knowledge
*“when the test was stolen the professor had to make a new set of questions”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **檢查 /檢查** — Particularly, an inspection by a medical professional to establish the extent and nature of any sickness or injury.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **考試 /考試, 測驗 /測驗** — A formal test involving answering written or oral questions under a time constraint and usually without access to textbooks; typically, a large, written test administered to high school and college students covering course material studied in a semester.

5. the act of examining something closely (as for mistakes)
6. formal systematic questioning

<a id="entry-e-002764"></a>
## examine

**發音：** /ɪɡˈzæmɪn/
*v.* 1. **研究** — consider in detail and subject to an analysis in order to discover essential features or meaning
*“analyze a sonnet by Shakespeare”*
*“analyze the evidence in a criminal trial”*
2. **細查, 調查, 檢查** — observe, check out, and look over carefully or inspect
*“The customs agent examined the baggage”*
*“I must see your passport before you can enter the country”*
3. **查究, 深入調查, 詳查, 探查, 細查** — question or examine thoroughly and closely
4. **追問** — question closely
5. **化驗, 測試, 試驗, 檢驗** — put to the test, as for its quality, or give experimental use to
*“This approach has been tried with good results”*
*“Test this recipe”*
6. *(transitive)* **檢查, 調查** — To observe carefully or critically to learn about someone or something.

<a id="entry-e-002786"></a>
## exception

*“all her children were brilliant; the only exception was her last child"”*
*“an exception tests the rule”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **排除** — The act of excepting or excluding; exclusion; restriction by taking out something which would otherwise be included, as in a class, statement, rule.
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **異常 /異常** — An interruption in normal processing, typically caused by an error condition, that can be raised (“thrown”) by one part of the program and handled (“caught”) by another part.
4. a deliberate act of omission
*“with the exception of the children, everyone was told the news”*
5. grounds for adverse criticism
*“his authority is beyond exception”*

<a id="entry-e-002787"></a>

*“special educational provisions for exceptional children”*

<a id="entry-e-002795"></a>
## exchange

**發音：** /ɛksˈt͡ʃeɪnd͡ʒ/
*v.* 1. **兌換, 更換, 調換, 交換** — give to, and receive from, one another
*“Would you change places with me?”*
*“We have been exchanging letters for a year”*
2. *(transitive)* **交換 /交換, 交流** — To trade or barter.
3. **掉換, 調換, 交換, 更換, 交易** — hand over one and receive another, approximately equivalent
*“exchange prisoners”*
*“exchange employees between branches of the company”*
4. **交替, 替, 替代, 代替, 替換** — put in the place of another; switch seemingly equivalent items
*“the con artist replaced the original with a fake Rembrandt”*
*“substitute regular milk with fat-free milk”*
5. exchange or replace with another, usually of the same kind or category
*“Could you convert my dollars into pounds?”*
*“He changed his name”*
6. change over, change around, as to a new order or sequence
*n.* 1. **交流** — a mutual expression of views (especially an unpleasant one)
*“they had a bitter exchange”*
2. **交換, 兌換, 調換** — the act of changing one thing for another thing
*“Adam was promised immortality in exchange for his disobedience”*
*“there was an interchange of prisoners”*
3. **電話交換臺, 電話局** — a workplace that serves as a telecommunications facility where lines from telephones can be connected together to permit communication
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **交換 /交換, 調換 /調換, 兌換 /兌換, 外匯 /外匯, 交流** — An act of exchanging or trading.
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **交易所** — A place for conducting trading.
6. **交易所** — a workplace for buying and selling; open only to members

<a id="entry-e-002852"></a>
## excuse

**發音：** /ɪkˈskjuːz/
*n.* 1. **自圓其說, 辯解, 藉口** — a defense of some offensive behavior or some failure to keep a promise etc.
*“he kept finding excuses to stay”*
*“every day he had a new alibi for not getting a job”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **藉口 /藉口, 辯解 /辯解** — An explanation designed to avoid or alleviate guilt or negative judgment; a plea offered in extenuation of a fault.
3. **藉口** — a poor example
*“it was an apology for a meal”*
*“a poor excuse for an automobile”*
4. a note explaining an absence
*“he had to get his mother to write an excuse for him”*
*v.* 1. **原諒, 寬恕, 饒恕** — accept an excuse for
*“Please excuse my dirty hands”*
2. **辯解, 辯護** — defend, explain, clear away, or make excuses for by reasoning
*“rationalize the child's seemingly crazy behavior”*
*“he rationalized his lack of success”*
3. *(transitive)* **原諒 /原諒** — To forgive; to pardon; to overlook.
4. *(transitive)* **請求離開 /請求離開** — To allow to leave, or release from any obligation.
5. *(transitive)* **辯解 /辯解** — To provide an excuse for; to justify.
6. grant exemption or release to
*“Please excuse me from this class”*

*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **現存 /現存** — existing; having life or being, current; occurring now

*“an existential moment of choice”*

3. **存在主義的 /存在主義的** — Of or relating to existentialism (“a philosophical movement emphasizing the uniqueness of each human existence in freely making its self-defining choices”).

*“formal logicians are not concerned with existential matters"- John Dewey”*

<a id="entry-e-002932"></a>

<a id="entry-e-003011"></a>
## experience

**發音：** /ɪkˈspɪə.ɹi.əns/
*n.* 1. **經歷, 經驗** — the accumulation of knowledge or skill that results from direct participation in events or activities
*“a man of experience”*
*“experience is the best teacher”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **經驗 /經驗, 體驗 /體驗** — The effect upon the judgment or feelings produced by any event, whether witnessed or participated in; personal and direct impressions as contrasted with description or fancies; personal acquaintance; actual enjoyment or suffering.
3. *(countable)* **經歷 /經歷** — An activity one has performed.
4. *(uncountable, usually)* **經歷 /經歷, 經驗 /經驗** — A collection of events and/or activities from which an individual or group may gather knowledge, opinions, and skills.
5. *(uncountable)* **經驗 /經驗** — The knowledge thus gathered.
6. the content of direct observation or participation in an event
*“he had a religious experience”*
*“he recalled the experience vividly”*
*v.* 1. **感受, 經驗, 體驗, 經歷** — go or live through
*“We had many trials to go through”*
*“he saw action in Viet Nam”*
2. **體驗, 感覺, 感, 感到** — undergo an emotional sensation or be in a particular state of mind
*“She felt resentful”*
*“He felt regret”*
3. *(transitive)* **經歷 /經歷, 體驗 /體驗** — To observe certain events; undergo a certain feeling or process; or perform certain actions that may alter one or contribute to one's knowledge, opinions, or skills.
4. have firsthand knowledge of states, situations, emotions, or sensations
*“I know the feeling!”*
*“have you ever known hunger?”*
5. go through (mental or physical states or experiences)
*“get an idea”*
*“experience vertigo”*
6. undergo
*“The stocks had a fast run-up”*

<a id="entry-e-003037"></a>
## explode

**發音：** /ɪkˈspləʊd/
*v.* 1. **推翻** — show a violent emotional reaction
*“The boss exploded when he heard of the resignation of the secretary”*
2. *(intransitive)* **爆炸, 爆發 /爆發** — To fly apart with sudden violent force; to blow up, to burst, to detonate, to go off.
3. cause to burst with a violent release of energy
*“We exploded the nuclear bomb”*
4. burst outward, usually with noise
*“The champagne bottle exploded”*
5. be unleashed; emerge with violence or noise

6. destroy by exploding

<a id="entry-e-003038"></a>

*“we must exploit the resources we are given wisely”*

*“Many of the laboratory animals express the trait”*

*v.* 1. **臨時做, 臨時湊成, 即興創作, 即興表演, 即席而作** — manage in a makeshift way; do with whatever is at hand
*“after the hurricane destroyed our house, we had to improvise for weeks”*
2. perform without preparation
*“he extemporized a speech at the wedding”*

<a id="entry-e-003103"></a>
## extend

**發音：** /ɛkˈstɛnd/
*v.* 1. **擴充, 變寬, 伸展, 擴大, 延伸** — extend in scope or range or area
*“The law was extended to all citizens”*
*“widen the range of applications”*
2. **延伸, 達到** — stretch out over a distance, space, time, or scope; run or extend between two points or beyond a certain point
*“Service runs all the way to Cranbury”*
*“His knowledge doesn't go very far”*
3. **延伸, 延續** — span an interval of distance, space or time
*“The war extended over five years”*
*“The period covered the turn of the century”*
4. **給予, 提供** — make available; provide
*“extend a loan”*
*“The bank offers a good deal on new mortgages”*
5. **延伸, 擴大, 伸展** — reach outward in space
*“The awning extends several feet over the sidewalk”*
6. **表示** — offer verbally
*“extend my greetings”*
*“He offered his sympathy”*

<a id="entry-e-003104"></a>
## extend to

*v.* 1. **伸出, 達到** — to extend as far as
*“The sunlight reached the wall”*
*“Can he reach?" "The chair must not touch the wall”*

<a id="entry-e-003105"></a>
## extended

*adj.* 1. **伸長+的, 延長+的, 擴大+的, 拉伸+的** — drawn out or made longer spatially
*“Picasso's elongated Don Quixote”*
*“lengthened skirts are fashionable this year”*
2. relatively long in duration; tediously protracted
*“a drawn-out argument”*
*“an extended discussion”*
3. fully extended or stretched forth
*“an extended telescope”*
*“his extended legs reached almost across the small room”*
4. beyond the literal or primary sense
*“`hot off the press' shows an extended sense of `hot'”*
5. large in spatial extent or range or scope or quantity
*“an extensive Roman settlement in northwest England”*
*“extended farm lands”*

*“across-the-board pay increases”*
*“an all-embracing definition”*

3. large in spatial extent or range or scope or quantity

## extinct

**發音：** /ɪkˈstɪŋkt/
*v.* 1. *(figuratively, obsolete, specifically)* **絕種 /絕種, 滅種 /滅種, 滅絕** — To cause (an animal or plant species) to die out completely or become extinct (adjective etymology 1 sense 2.3).
*adj.* 1. **滅絕+的, 熄滅+的, 耗盡+的** — no longer in existence; lost or especially having died out leaving no living representatives
*“an extinct species of fish”*

*“an extinct volcano”*

*“threw his extinct cigarette into the stream”*

<a id="entry-e-003135"></a>
## extinction

**發音：** /ɪkˈstɪŋkʃən/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **滅絕 /滅絕** — The action of making or becoming extinct; annihilation.
2. **消滅, 消亡, 滅絕, 毀滅** — no longer active; extinguished
*“the extinction of the volcano”*
3. **消亡, 滅絕, 絕種** — no longer in existence
*“the extinction of a species”*
4. **消退** — the reduction of the intensity of radiation as a consequence of absorption and radiation
5. complete annihilation
*“they think a meteor cause the extinction of the dinosaurs”*
6. a conditioning process in which the reinforcer is removed and a conditioned response becomes independent of the conditioned stimulus

<a id="entry-e-003136"></a>
## extinguish

**發音：** /ɪkˈstɪŋ.ɡwɪʃ/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **熄滅 /熄滅, 撲滅 /撲滅** — To stop (fire, etc.) from burning; also, to stop (light, etc.) from shining; to put out, to quench.
2. **撲滅, 熄火, 熄滅** — put out, as of fires, flames, or lights
*“Too big to be extinguished at once, the forest fires at best could be contained”*
*“quench the flames”*
3. **幹掉, 消除, 除去, 除掉, 擺脫** — terminate, end, or take out
*“Let's eliminate the course on Akkadian hieroglyphics”*
*“Socialism extinguished these archaic customs”*
4. put an end to; kill
*“The Nazis snuffed out the life of many Jewish children”*

6. kill in large numbers
*“the plague wiped out an entire population”*

## extract

**發音：** /ˈɛkstɹækt/
*v.* 1. **拔出, 提取, 提煉出, 取出** — remove, usually with some force or effort; also used in an abstract sense
*“pull weeds”*
*“extract a bad tooth”*
2. **吸取, 選粹** — get despite difficulties or obstacles
*“I extracted a promise from the Dean for two new positions”*
3. deduce (a principle) or construe (a meaning)
*“We drew out some interesting linguistic data from the native informant”*
4. extract by the process of distillation
*“distill the essence of this compound”*
5. separate (a metal) from an ore
6. obtain from a substance, as by mechanical action
*“Italians express coffee rather than filter it”*

*adj.* 1. **顯著+的, 超出一般或平常的, 了不起+的, 非凡+的, 特別+的** — beyond what is ordinary or usual; highly unusual or exceptional or remarkable
*“extraordinary authority”*
*“an extraordinary achievement”*
2. **非凡, 了不起, 出眾 /出眾** — Out of the ordinary; exceptional; unusual.
3. far more than usual or expected
*“an extraordinary desire for approval”*
*“it was an over-the-top experience”*

<a id="entry-e-003169"></a>
## extrapolate

**發音：** /ɛkˈstɹæp.əˌleɪt/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **外推** — To infer by extending known information.
2. *(transitive)* **外推** — To estimate the value of a variable outside a known range from values within that range by assuming that the estimated value follows logically from the known ones
3. draw from specific cases for more general cases
4. estimate the value of
5. gain knowledge of (an area not known or experienced) by extrapolating

<a id="entry-e-003170"></a>
## extrapolation

2. *(countable, uncountable)* **眼疲勞 /眼疲勞, 眼睛疲勞 /眼睛疲勞, 視疲勞 /視疲勞, 視覺疲勞 /視覺疲勞** — Tiredness or pain in the eyes, sometimes accompanied by headache, caused by excessive or improper use of the eyes, or by uncorrected defects of vision.

## ezafe

**發音：** /ɛˈzɑːfeɪ/
*n.* 1. **耶扎菲** — A grammatical particle found in some Iranian and Iranian-influenced languages which links two words together; it consists of the unstressed vowel -e / -ye (Persian, Bengali, Hindi-Urdu, Punjabi), -i / -yi (Classical Persian, Kurdish, Bengali, Hindi-Urdu, Punjabi, Ottoman Turkish), or -ı (Ottoman Turkish) between the words it connects, and often approximately corresponds in usage to the English preposition "of", similar to but not to be confused with Arabic ʾiḍāfa.
